- [Claude Science](entries/claude-science.md) — Anthropic's AI workbench for scientists — generalist coordinating agent with 60+ curated skills for genomics/proteomics/structural-biology/cheminformatics, native 3D molecular rendering, HPC/GPU compute management via Modal, reviewer agent for citation/calculation checking, NVIDIA BioNeMo integration (Evo 2, Boltz-2, OpenFold3); runs on existing Claude models; Pro/Max/Team/Enterprise beta
- [Claw Code](entries/claw-code.md) — Rust reimplementation of a CLI agent harness; self-described museum exhibit maintained by agents rather than a production tool
- [Cline](entries/cline.md) — Open-source autonomous coding agent distributed as a VS Code extension, JetBrains plugin (early access), SDK, and CLI — file editing, terminal command execution, browser automation, and MCP tool integration with human-in-the-loop approval by default (diff review, checkpoints to undo agent work, optional auto-approve); project rules via .clinerules files; MCP Marketplace and hosted SaaS (Cline.bot) with free/paid/enterprise tiers; developed by Cline Bot Inc.; TypeScript; ~71k GitHub stars, 5M+ VS Code installs; Apache-2.0
- [CLM (Contrastive Language Model)](entries/clm.md) — Open-weights, self-hostable classification model positioned as a drop-in alternative to TypeSafe AI's Jev — a frozen Qwen3-8B encoder plus two 20M-param trainable projection heads (state/action) trained with bidirectional InfoNCE contrastive loss; serves the same three typed-question primitives (Choice, Noul, Score) behind a TypeSafe-compatible API; reference head is ~75MB (Apache-2.0); reports up to 9x lower zero-shot latency and, with lightweight fine-tuning, SOTA verifier accuracy on Terminal-Bench 2.1 (87.6%) and DeepSWE (81.6%) at 4-6x Jev's speed
- [Cloudflare Clef](entries/cloudflare-clef.md) — Open-weight decision models from Cloudflare — Clef (27B, Qwen 3.8-27B backbone, 209ms median) and Clef-flash (9B, Qwen 3.5-9B backbone, 39ms median); return typed probabilities instead of text; 64K context; vision encoder (up to 4 images); RL fine-tuning platform on Workers AI; beats Jev on 7/10 benchmarks; Apache 2.0
- [Cloudflare OS](entries/cloudflare-os.md) — Cloudflare's open-sourced internal 'agent workspace' platform on Workers/Durable Objects — sandboxed per-user 'Gadgets' (AI-built mini-apps) governed by capability-based 'Gatekeeper' services that mediate external-API access and support async human-in-the-loop approval
- [colibri](entries/colibri.md) — Pure-C, zero-dependency inference engine that runs very large open-weight mixture-of-experts (MoE) LLMs (up to ~2.8T parameters) on ordinary hardware by keeping the small per-token-active dense part resident in RAM/VRAM and streaming the inactive routed experts from disk; ships a `coli` CLI, OpenAI- and Anthropic-compatible HTTP API, an MCP server for AI-assisted setup, and a non-generative "System One" classification endpoint backed by three purpose-built decision models
- [ComfyUI](entries/comfyui.md) — Node-graph GUI, API, and backend for diffusion/generative-media models (Python/PyTorch) — build image/video/audio/3D pipelines visually from modular nodes (samplers, ControlNet, LoRA, upscalers), with smart partial re-execution, low-VRAM offloading, workflows saved as reproducible JSON (embedded in generated PNGs), a headless API mode, custom-node ecosystem via ComfyUI-Manager, and a cross-platform desktop app; GPL-3.0, ~132k stars, Comfy-Org backed
- [Conductor OSS](entries/conductor-oss.md) — Open-source durable execution platform (ex-Netflix) for microservices, AI agents, and adaptive workflow graphs — JSON-defined orchestration with durable state, native LLM tasks, MCP tool calling, human approval, dynamic fork/join, DO_WHILE loops, polyglot workers (Java/Python/Go/JS/C#/Ruby/Rust), 5 persistence backends, 6 message brokers; Claude Code plugin; Apache-2.0
- [Coolify](entries/coolify.md) — Coolify manages servers, applications, and databases on your own hardware via SSH, providing a web dashboard for git-push deployments, one-click database provisioning, preview deployments, and rollback; self-hosted and has had several critical CVEs in 2025/2026
- [Crawl4AI](entries/crawl4ai.md) — Best-in-class async web-to-Markdown crawler for agent/RAG pipelines, but Docker API has had dense critical CVEs; library mode is safe to pilot
- [CReM](entries/crem.md) — CReM generates chemically valid molecular structures by swapping, adding, or linking fragments that share the same chemical context, via four modes — mutate, grow, link, and make-cycle — scoring competitively on the GuacaMol goal-directed benchmark
- [CrewAI](entries/crewai.md) — Lean Python multi-agent orchestration framework — role-based Crews for autonomous collaboration + event-driven Flows for precise control; MIT, 100K+ certified devs, Claude Code skills plugin, independent of LangChain
- [Crossplane](entries/crossplane.md) — CNCF framework for building cloud-native control planes without writing code: an extensible Kubernetes-based backend that orchestrates applications and infrastructure wherever they run, plus a configurable frontend that lets you define the schema of the declarative API it offers. v2.x is current (v2.3, May 2026); v1.20 is the final v1 minor release on extended critical-fix-only support.
- [CrowdSec](entries/crowdsec.md) — Production-ready crowdsourced IDS/IPS framework — valuable for hardening servers hosting agent infrastructure, outside direct agent/bioinformatics workflows.
- [CuPy](entries/cupy.md) — NumPy/SciPy-compatible Python array library for GPU-accelerated computing — drop-in replacement on NVIDIA CUDA (12.x/13.x) and AMD ROCm 7.0 with low-level CUDA access (RawKernels, Streams, Runtime APIs); includes cuSignal; 11K stars, 127 contributors, MIT, Preferred Networks
- [CustomTkinter](entries/customtkinter.md) — Python UI library built on Tkinter providing modern, customizable widgets (CTkButton, CTkFrame, scrollable frames) with light/dark appearance modes, HighDPI scaling, and JSON-based theming across Windows, macOS, and Linux
- [Databasement](entries/databasement.md) — Self-hosted database backup manager with web UI — schedule, backup, and restore MySQL, PostgreSQL, MariaDB, SQL Server, MongoDB, SQLite, Firebird, and Redis to S3/SFTP/Samba/Azure Blob/local storage; SSH tunnels, remote agents, GFS retention, AES-256 encryption, cross-server restore, scheduled restores, built-in Adminer browser, multi-tenant RBAC with OAuth/SSO, REST API and MCP server; single Docker container; MIT
- [Deep-Live-Cam](entries/deep-live-cam.md) — Real-time face swap and video deepfake from a single image — webcam live mode, multi-face mapping, mouth mask, GPU acceleration (CUDA/CoreML/DirectML/OpenVINO); built on InsightFace/GFPGAN; viral 50K+ stars but no stated SPDX license and uses non-commercial research models
- [DeepSeek Harness (dsh)](entries/deepseek-harness.md) — Open-source agent harness from DeepSeek AI built on Cordis, a reversible plugin meta-framework — models, tools, skills, sessions, sandboxes, storage, loops, scheduling, and the UI are all swappable plugins composed via configuration. MIT-licensed developer preview, open-sourced August 13, 2026 alongside DeepSeek-V4-Pro; quick start is `npx @deepseek-ai/dsh web`.
- [DiffDock](entries/diffdock.md) — DiffDock predicts protein-ligand binding poses with a generative diffusion model operating over the SE(3) space of ligand translations, rotations, and torsion angles, ranking candidate poses by a learned confidence score instead of scoring pre-sampled poses
- [Dify](entries/dify.md) — Dify provides a visual drag-and-drop interface for building AI applications, combining workflow orchestration, RAG document ingestion/retrieval, agent tool use with 50+ built-in tools, model management, and production observability (LLMOps) into a single self-hostable platform
- [Documenso](entries/documenso.md) — Open-source DocuSign alternative for digital document signing — self-hostable TypeScript/React Router v7/Hono/Prisma stack with PDF signature support (@libpdf/core), i18n, Stripe billing, E2E tests (Playwright); Docker/K8s/Railway deployment; AGPL-3.0
- [DrEvalPy](entries/drevalpy.md) — Python cancer cell line drug response prediction benchmark suite — maintained baseline model catalog, gold standard datasets (CTRPv2, GDSC1/2, CCLE, BeatAML2), application-driven evaluation splits (LPO/LCO/LDO/LTO), ablation studies, cross-study generalization, nf-core Nextflow pipeline; Nature Comms published, TUM/FU Berlin
- [DrugEx](entries/drugex.md) — DrugEx generates novel drug-like small molecules using deep learning generative models within a multi-objective reinforcement learning framework, supporting RNN, SMILES-transformer, and scaffold-constrained graph-transformer generator architectures with Pareto-based multi-objective optimization
- [DSPy](entries/dspy.md) — Stanford NLP framework for programming (not prompting) LLMs via typed signatures, composable modules, and automatic prompt optimization; 35k stars, 433+ contributors, production use at scale — relevant for building optimized LLM pipelines
- [DuckDB](entries/duckdb.md) — High-performance in-process analytical (OLAP) SQL database. Rich SQL dialect (window functions, correlated subqueries, nested types), zero-config, embeddable; runs as a standalone CLI and as clients for Python, R, Java, Wasm, etc., with direct querying of CSV/Parquet files and pandas/dplyr integration. MIT.
- [E2B](entries/e2b.md) — Open-source cloud sandbox infrastructure for AI agents — Firecracker microVMs with ~150ms boot, Python/JS SDKs, Code Interpreter, self-hostable on AWS/GCP via Terraform; Apache-2.0, 8.9K stars
- [ECC — Everything Claude Code (GitHub)](entries/ecc-github.md) — massive cross-harness skill/hook/agent system worth monitoring but high adoption complexity and scope overlap with existing stack
- [ElevenLabs](entries/elevenlabs.md) — Commercial AI voice platform — text-to-speech (TTS), voice cloning, and voice-agent APIs; models adapt intonation/pacing/emotion to textual cues across up to 32 languages (marketing cites 90+ languages and 17,500+ voices for the broader product); voice library of 10,000+ community-shared voices plus professional and instant voice cloning and text-described voice design; core endpoint `POST /v1/text-to-speech/:voice_id` (model_id defaults to eleven_multilingual_v2, optional language_code); Eleven Realtime bundles ASR + VAD + turn-taking + LLM + TTS into one speech-to-speech voice agent; official JS (`@elevenlabs/elevenlabs-js`) and Python SDKs plus community integrations (Vercel AI SDK, LangChain-style wrappers); closed-source hosted SaaS, $5-$990+/mo tiers, ~$0.30/1k characters API metering; privacy policy retains voice-model data up to 3 years post-interaction with no published deletion guarantee for a trained voice-clone model, trains on consumer-tier data by default (opt-out available), and faces an active 2026 biometric-data class action and recurring deepfake/voice-cloning-misuse reports; Enterprise tier adds SOC 2 Type II, HIPAA, GDPR terms, EU residency, and a traffic-limited Zero Retention Mode
- [EmbeddingGemma 2](entries/embeddinggemma-2.md) — Google DeepMind's 740M-parameter open multimodal embedding model — maps text, code, images, video, and audio into one 768-dim vector space; modular encoders (270M text-only to 740M full); 8K context window; runs on-device (191MB RAM for text-only on Pixel 11 Pro); Matryoshka truncation to 512/256/128 dims; built on Gemma 4; Apache 2.0
- [emdly](entries/emdly.md) — Platform for discovering, comparing, and sharing SKILL.md files for AI agents — curated marketplace with human review and sandboxed safety screening; CLI installer (npx @emdly/cli add), synced to public GitHub repo (emdly-stack); ~24+ skills across engineering (PR review, debugging, commit messages, dependency upgrades), analytics (GA4 funnels, SQL explainer, dashboard metrics), content (SEO briefs, brand voice, landing pages), ops (incident postmortem, K8s triage, standup synthesis), commerce (product feeds, cart sequences), and gaming (balance review, playtest triage)
- [ENGRAFT](entries/engraft-ngram.md) — Technique for writing facts into an LLM's n-gram (Engram) lookup table by gradient descent on table rows only — no weight changes, one removable overlay file; measured on Qwen3.8-Flash-Next (320M-row table); 84.1% exact-match on 100 invented facts, 0.013 KL divergence on neutral text; gradient runs on CPU replica, verified on llama.cpp GGUF; early-stage research
- [ERA — Empirical Research Assistant](entries/era-empirical-research-assistant.md) — Interesting LLM+tree-search loop for scientific code generation, but Gemini-locked, research-grade, and not directly applicable to Claude Code workflows today
- [Event2Vector (event2vec)](entries/event2vector.md) — Python/PyTorch library for interpretable, composable embeddings of discrete event sequences — learns one vector per event type and represents a sequence as the composition (vector sum under the linear additive hypothesis) of its events, so trajectories support word2vec-style arithmetic; geometries include Euclidean, hyperbolic (Möbius addition), spherical, stereographic, rotation, Heisenberg, affine, and product; scikit-learn-style fit/transform/most_similar API; `pip install event2vector`; MIT; paper in PMLR vol. 282 (arXiv:2509.12188)
- [FAISS](entries/faiss.md) — Meta's C++/Python library for efficient similarity search and clustering of dense vectors — billion-scale datasets, GPU acceleration (CUDA/ROCm), multiple index types (HNSW, NSG, IVF, PQ), L2/dot-product/cosine similarity; 40K+ stars, MIT
- [FastAPI](entries/fastapi.md) — High-performance async Python web framework built on Starlette + Pydantic — declares request/response contracts via standard Python type hints, generating validation, serialization, and OpenAPI/JSON Schema docs (Swagger UI, ReDoc) automatically; dependency-injection system, WebSockets, background tasks; MIT, ~102k stars, created 2018 by Sebastián Ramírez, actively maintained
- [Faster Whisper](entries/faster-whisper.md) — CTranslate2-based Whisper reimplementation — 4x faster than openai/whisper at same accuracy, int8 quantization, batched inference, VAD filtering, word-level timestamps; core ASR runtime for caption-less audio/video ingestion
- [Firecracker](entries/firecracker.md) — AWS-developed open-source VMM (KVM-based) that creates minimal-overhead 'microVMs' for secure multi-tenant workload isolation — ~125ms boot, <5MiB overhead per VM; powers every AWS Lambda/Fargate invocation and underlies sandbox platforms like E2B and Vercel Sandbox; Apache-2.0, Rust
- [Firecrawl](entries/firecrawl.md) — Production-grade web scraping/search/interaction API for AI agents — clean markdown output, JS rendering, anti-bot handling, Agent endpoint with Spark models, Map/Interact/Batch Scrape, MCP server mode; 9 SDKs, AGPL-3.0, YC-backed
- [Flectar Mail](entries/flectar-mail.md) — Native Rust email, calendar, and contacts client marketed on low memory use (README: as little as 80 MB RAM) and a no-WebView, no-script HTML rendering pipeline (Blitz); pre-release, AGPLv3.
- [Floci — Local Cloud Emulators](entries/floci.md) — Local cloud emulators for AWS (119 services, port 4566), Azure (28 services), GCP (25 services), and OCI (8 services) — native binaries compiled with GraalVM Mandrel, 24ms cold start, 13 MiB idle; drop-in LocalStack replacement (same port, zero code changes); real engines (Lambda in Docker, RDS with real PostgreSQL/MySQL, ElastiCache with real Redis); no auth tokens, no accounts, no telemetry; unified CLI and visual dashboard; MIT
- [Free Claude Code](entries/free-claude-code.md) — Drop-in proxy routing Claude Code (Anthropic Messages API) and Codex (OpenAI Responses API) traffic to 24 provider backends — per-model-tier routing, Admin UI, Discord/Telegram bot wrapper, voice-note transcription via local Whisper or NVIDIA NIM; Python/FastAPI, MIT
- [FreeToken](entries/freetoken.md) — Edge-native MoE inference engine from UC Berkeley/UT Austin — runs 290B+ frontier MoE models on consumer hardware by keeping experts in system RAM and LRU-caching active experts to GPU; bandwidth-adaptive Q* scheduling, double-buffered prefill streaming, semantic anchor checkpoints for agentic context reuse, elastic VRAM reallocation; 3-4x faster decode and 6-30x faster prefill vs Ollama/llama.cpp on VRAM-oversubscribed MoE workloads; OpenAI/Anthropic-compatible API; desktop app + CLI; supports RTX 30/40/50 series; Apache-2.0
- [gbrain](entries/gbrain.md) — kb wiki already provides persistent typed records with relationships and search
- [Gemini 3.8 Flash](entries/gemini-3-8-flash.md) — Google DeepMind's Gemini 3 family Flash-tier model (built on Gemini 3.7 Flash), published 2026-09-02 — 1M-token multimodal input context (text/image/audio/video), 64K text output, customizable inference effort levels, $0.75/$3.75 per 1M input/output tokens; targets cost-effective software engineering and agentic knowledge-work at production scale
- [Gemini 4 Argon (Google)](entries/gemini-4-argon.md) — Google's newest frontier LLM, announced 2026-09-30 and broadly rolled out 2026-10-13 — built for long-horizon coding and agent tasks, up to 1M output tokens (vs a prior 64K limit), Google Antigravity agentic coding interface integration, and cybersecurity-defense tuning; available via the Gemini app (AI Pro/Ultra), the API in AI Studio/Gemini platform, and Google Cloud; Google reports a 14% enterprise-knowledge-work accuracy gain over Gemini 3.1 Pro and a drop in coding shortcut-attempts to 2%; introductory API pricing $2/$10 per 1M input/output tokens through 2026-11-30
- [GENTRL](entries/gentrl.md) — GENTRL generates novel small molecules optimized for synthetic feasibility, biological activity, and novelty using a variational autoencoder with a tensor-decomposition-parameterized learned prior, trained in a pretrain-then-RL-optimize two-stage pipeline
- [GeoLibre](entries/geolibre.md) — Lightweight, cloud-native open-source GIS platform (opengeos) for visualizing, exploring, and analyzing geospatial data locally. Built on Tauri v2, React, MapLibre GL JS, DuckDB-WASM Spatial, and deck.gl; the same workspace runs as a native desktop app, native Android app, in the browser, and inside Jupyter, with 700+ browser-side GIS tools and Earth + planetary basemaps. MIT.
- [Get Shit Done (GSD)](entries/get-shit-done.md) — deprecated (moved to GSD Redux); superpowers skill system already covers meta-prompting and spec-driven development
- [Get Started with AI Chat (Azure AI Foundry)](entries/azure-ai-foundry-chat-starter.md) — Microsoft starter template that deploys a RAG-capable chat web app to Azure Container Apps using Microsoft Foundry models and Azure AI Search for file-grounded, cited responses; deployable via azd up or a guided Copilot CLI /up skill; explicitly marked not production-ready without added security hardening
- [ggwas](entries/ggwas.md) — Modern ggplot2 GWAS visualization R package — 17 plot types (Manhattan, QQ, Miami, PheWAS, colocalization, fine-mapping, genetic correlations, karyogram, enrichment), journal themes (Nature/Science/Cell/PLOS), smart downsampling for 10M+ variants, auto-detects PLINK/REGENIE/GCTA/GEMMA; MIT
- [Ghidra](entries/ghidra.md) — Open-source software reverse engineering (SRE) framework from NSA's Research Directorate — disassembly, decompilation, graphing, and scripting for compiled code on Windows, macOS, and Linux
- [God's Eye View](entries/gods-eye-view.md) — Browser-based open-source spatial-intelligence globe (vanilla JS + CesiumJS + Vite) fusing live public feeds — 11k+ aircraft, ships, satellites, earthquakes, fires, ~800 public CCTV cameras, radio, launches — onto a photorealistic 3D Earth, with an OpenAI Realtime voice agent (28 tools) for hands-free control; keyless baseline, MIT per README; ~24k stars, #1 GitHub Trending Aug 2026
- [Google Cloud Knowledge Catalog](entries/google-knowledge-catalog.md) — Google Cloud's Gemini-powered data catalog and metadata management platform (formerly Dataplex); builds a dynamic context graph of structured/unstructured data assets for AI agent grounding, with MCP server integration for context retrieval
- [Gradio](entries/gradio.md) — Gradio lets developers create interactive web UIs for machine learning models, APIs, or arbitrary Python functions in a few lines of code, with a FastAPI backend, Svelte frontend, programmatic Python/JS clients, and free Hugging Face Spaces hosting
- [Graphify](entries/graphify.md) — direct inspiration for kb graph; concepts like confidence-tagged edges and clustering worth learning from
- [GROMACS](entries/gromacs.md) — GROMACS is a widely used molecular dynamics simulation package for proteins, lipids, and nucleic acids, optimized through multi-level parallelism (SIMD, GPU offloading via CUDA/OpenCL/SYCL, and MPI) with support for AMBER/CHARMM/GROMOS/OPLS force fields and free-energy calculations
- [gstack](entries/gstack.md) — kb dispatch covers the relevant orchestration patterns; browser/deployment out of scope
- [gufo](entries/gufo.md) — Source-built local inference engine for AMD Strix Halo hardware (Ryzen AI Max+ 395, Radeon 8060S/gfx1151, up to 128GB unified memory) — serves GGUF models (documented against Qwen3.8-Flash-Next and Qwen3.8-27B) over an OpenAI-compatible API with MTP or DFlash2 speculative decoding and vision via a separate mmproj file; Anthropic-style /v1/messages endpoint does not yet support tool calls or streaming; MIT, requires a ROCm toolchain to build
- [Halogen (halogen-flash-server)](entries/halogen-flash-server.md) — Closed-source, hardware-specific local inference server for running Qwen3.8-Flash-Next on AMD Strix Halo (gfx1151) silicon — distributed as a container image behind an open deployment-tooling repo; serves OpenAI- and Anthropic-compatible APIs on one port, supports MTP speculative decoding, and can route small models to the Ryzen AI NPU alongside the main model; the engine binary itself is proprietary, licensed by Peonist, LLC, separately from the public deployment scaffolding
- [HarnessX](entries/harnessx.md) — Python agent-harness foundry from the Darwin Agent Team separating model (ModelConfig) from behavior (HarnessConfig): a 9-dimension processor pipeline (context, control, evaluation, memory, multi-model, observability, tools, plus providers/plugins) composed with a `|` operator, plus two self-reported evolution loops — a meta-harness that auto-searches processor/config combinations, and RL fine-tuning (via VERL) on reward-annotated run trajectories. MIT-licensed, v0.1.0/Beta. Companion papers: HarnessX (arXiv:2606.14249) and a follow-on, DarwinX (arXiv:2608.07545).
- [Harness² (Google Research)](entries/harness2.md) — Google Research framework for recursive agent harnessing — the agent refines its own harness (system prompt, guardrails, memory, tool guidance, subagents, skills, scripts, plugins) per task via a Propose–Probe–Compose loop that compares candidate harness versions without graders or reference answers, then carries adopted edits and lessons forward in a persistent improvement harness; model weights unchanged; runs OpenCode/Codex executors on GLM-5 and Gemini 3.5 Flash; Python 3.12, Apache-2.0 (arXiv:2610.14224)
- [Headroom](entries/headroom.md) — Powerful context compression with real benchmarks, but proxy/MITM position and headroom learn's CLAUDE.md writes are significant trust risks for adoption without review
- [HNSWlib](entries/hnswlib.md) — Header-only C++ library (with Python bindings) implementing HNSW (Hierarchical Navigable Small World) graph search — the standalone reference implementation of the HNSW algorithm by Yury Malkov and Dmitry Yashunin; incremental index construction, element deletion/update, custom C++ distances, L2/inner-product/cosine spaces; Apache-2.0
- [Hugging Face Inference Providers](entries/huggingface-inference-providers.md) — Unified API gateway routing inference to 15+ backend providers (Groq, Together AI, Cerebras, Replicate, etc.) via a single HF token; OpenAI-compatible, multi-task (LLM, VLM, image gen, embeddings, speech), with provider selection policies (:fastest, :cheapest, :preferred)
- [HyperFrames](entries/hyperframes.md) — Open-source, agent-native HTML-to-video rendering system by HeyGen — "write HTML, render video"; seek-based deterministic rendering (window.__hf object exposing duration + seek()) captured headlessly via Puppeteer/CDP and encoded with FFmpeg; three-method FrameAdapter interface (init/getDurationFrames/seekFrame) plugs in GSAP, CSS/WAAPI, Lottie, Three.js, Anime.js, or a custom runtime, so e.g. Three.js scenes seek on hf-seek events and a window.__hfThreeTime clock instead of wall-clock time; CLI (`npx hyperframes init`) plus modular packages (core, engine, producer, browser studio editor, embeddable player, aws-lambda distributed render); migration skill from Remotion/Motion Canvas/Lottie/canvas verified frame by frame; installs as a Claude Code plugin-marketplace entry or standalone skill (`npx skills add heygen-com/hyperframes`); Node.js 22+ and FFmpeg required; Apache-2.0; ~75.5k stars/6.9k forks
- [iai — Personal Memory Engine](entries/iai-personal-memory-engine.md) — Fully local MCP-based personal memory engine for AI assistants — ambient capture, three-tier memory (episodic/semantic/procedural), custom storage engine (Hippo), clustering (MOSAIC), HD substrate (LilliHD), Rust embedder; macOS-only, English-only, MIT
- [imageio-ffmpeg](entries/imageio-ffmpeg.md) — Pure-Python FFmpeg subprocess wrapper that reads/writes video frames via pipes as generator functions, bundling platform-specific ffmpeg binaries in its wheels; maintainers now recommend PyAV instead for speed and features
- [Immich](entries/immich.md) — Production-ready self-hosted Google Photos alternative with AI search; not agent tooling but solid self-hosted media infrastructure.
- [In-game Debug Console (UnityIngameDebugConsole)](entries/ingame-debug-console.md) — yasirkula's uGUI-based in-game console for Unity — shows runtime log/warning/error/exception output and lets players or testers execute registered commands (e.g. cheats) from a resizable, minimizable window inside a running build, including on mobile/device builds where the editor console isn't available; supports Android logcat forwarding; 2.8k stars, 268 forks; MIT.
- [Inbox Zero](entries/inbox-zero.md) — AI-powered self-hostable email management app — strong open-source email assistant, not a developer/agent toolkit.
- [Jantt](entries/jantt.md) — TypeScript Gantt chart engine that renders interactive, draggable timelines from a declarative JSON schema — zero runtime dependencies, under 14KB gzipped core; ships as @jantt/core, @jantt/react, @jantt/standalone (UMD/IIFE script tag), and a jantt CLI with bidirectional file-watch sync; includes a topological DAG solver, Critical Path Method analysis, PERT estimation, Earned Value Management (ANSI/EIA-748), and DCMA-14 schedule audits; optional real-time multi-user collaboration backed by Firebase Realtime Database + Cloudflare Workers, gated behind GitHub OAuth and a "follow and star the maintainer's repos" unlock; MIT license, single maintainer (AhmadHassan-BTed), ~155 commits, 339 passing tests
- [JAX](entries/jax.md) — Python library for accelerator-oriented array computing and composable function transformations: jax.grad (autodiff to any order, forward/reverse), jax.jit (XLA compilation to CPU/GPU/TPU), and jax.vmap (auto-vectorization), plus sharding APIs to scale across thousands of devices. Apache-2.0; a research project, not an official Google product.
- [jcode](entries/jcode.md) — Feature-rich Rust coding agent harness with exceptional performance (14ms boot, 28MB RAM), swarm collaboration, and 30+ provider integrations; compelling alternative harness to watch
- [Jev (TypeSafe AI)](entries/jev.md) — Non-generative 'System One' classification model from TypeSafe AI — evaluates state and returns typed answers with calibrated probabilities (choice, score, noul/boolean) instead of generating text; trained with RLCD; reports up to 200x faster inference and 400x lower cost than LLMs on classification tasks; parallel multi-question evaluation per request; LangChain integration via langchain-typesafe; use cases include model routing, agent guardrails (auto-mode safety classification), and ticket triage
- [Jina-OCR-v1](entries/jina-ocr-v1.md) — End-to-end document-to-Markdown OCR model (3.4B total / 570M active MoE) with FastMTP speculative decoding (K=3, ~2x decode speed on L4) — scores 91.14 OmniDocBench, 83.4 olmOCR-Bench, 2.57 pages/s on A100; handles text, tables, math, handwriting, 100+ languages; runs via Transformers or vLLM; hosted via Jina Reader API; CC BY-NC 4.0 (weights)
- [JIT-Agent (Just-in-Time Harness Evolution)](entries/jit-agent.md) — Research meta-agent (arXiv 2608.25593) that generates a task-specific agent harness on the fly — given a task spec, protocol, and tool registry it emits executable harness code factored into memory/planning/action/capability modules against shared HarnessFactory interfaces, revises it from execution traces, and selects among N candidates by judge or logprob; ships a JIT-27B meta-model checkpoint and 7 benchmark adapters; Python, ~440 stars
- [Jive](entries/jive.md) — Terminal coding-agent harness (TypeScript/Bun) whose planner submits one DAG ('graph call') of bash and Jev-decision nodes per turn instead of one tool call at a time, executing the plan locally and returning to the LLM only when a node's accept predicate fails; author-published, single-run benchmarks across 6 tasks report a mean 2m31s/8.7k output tokens for Jive vs 12m12s/32.7k tokens for Claude Code and ~17m40s/16.2k tokens for Codex. MIT license, single contributor, repo created 2026-09-21.
- [Karakeep](entries/karakeep.md) — Self-hostable AI bookmark manager with REST API, official agent skills, and Ollama support — agent-friendly but self-described as under heavy development.
- [kb (Repo-Local Wiki + Agent Dispatch + Graph)](entries/kb.md) — Agent-facing toolkit providing a structured repo-local wiki (MCP + CLI), reviewed multi-agent dispatch with sandboxed launches and write-scope enforcement, and deterministic code-first graph extraction; runs from its own checkout and targets consuming repos via --dir; MCP servers for wiki (10 tools) and dispatch (9 tools); MIT
- [Knowledge Work Plugins (Anthropic)](entries/knowledge-work-plugins.md) — A marketplace of 11 open-source Claude plugins that turn Claude into a role-specific specialist (productivity, sales, customer-support, product-management, marketing, legal, finance, data, enterprise-search, bio-research), each bundling MCP connectors, slash commands, and domain skills
- [LanceDB](entries/lancedb.md) — Embedded serverless vector database built on Lance columnar format — runs in-process with no server, IVF/HNSW/PQ/RQ vector indexes + BM25 full-text, multimodal columns (text/image/video/audio), Git-style versioning, zero-copy reads, object storage backend (S3/GCS/AZ), DuckDB SQL integration, Python/TypeScript/Rust/REST SDKs; Apache-2.0
- [LangChain](entries/langchain.md) — Open-source (MIT) LLM application framework providing composable components — chains, runnables, tools, retrievers, prompts — for building model-agnostic agents; as of 2026, its create_agent ReAct-pattern agents run on LangGraph's durable runtime, inheriting persistence, checkpointing, rewind, and human-in-the-loop support rather than implementing orchestration independently.
- [Langflow](entries/langflow.md) — Heavy visual-builder platform with native MCP-server export; too large for direct adoption but worth monitoring for MCP workflow integration patterns.
- [LangGraph](entries/langgraph.md) — Open-source (MIT) low-level agent-orchestration engine built around a directed graph of nodes and edges over shared state — supports loops, conditional routing, and multi-agent coordination as first-class constructs, with persisted state that survives steps (resumable from the last successful node, step-level checkpointing, human-in-the-loop interrupts). LangChain's own agents run on this runtime as of 2026 rather than a separate execution model; LangGraph itself runs standalone without LangChain's components.
- [LangSmith](entries/langsmith.md) — LangChain's commercial observability, evaluation, and deployment platform for LLM/agent applications — traces, evals, and monitoring that work against almost any stack via OpenTelemetry, not just LangChain/LangGraph. The client SDK (langsmith-sdk) is MIT-licensed, but the platform backend, UI, and storage layer are closed-source and unpublished; self-hosting requires an Enterprise plan and a sales-issued license key. In May 2026, LangChain replaced LangSmith's ClickHouse storage engine on its US Cloud with SmithDB, a proprietary Rust engine on Apache DataFusion and Vortex; self-hosted deployments still run ClickHouse+Postgres, with SmithDB there in a waitlisted public beta that LangChain does not recommend self-operating.
- [Laya](entries/laya-decision-model.md) — Open-source non-autoregressive decision model from Convai Innovations — 421M params (ModernBERT-large backbone) English, 322M multilingual (100+ languages); single forward pass in ~33-40ms on T4; three primitives (choice, score, noul); accuracy 0.766 vs Jev's 0.727 on typed-decisions benchmark at 7.8x lower latency; self-hosted, zero marginal cost; Apache 2.0
- [Laya (Convai Innovations)](entries/laya.md) — Multilingual, non-autoregressive 'System 1' decision model family from Convai Innovations — takes a state (text/email/ticket/JSON) plus typed questions (choice/score/noul) and returns typed answers with calibrated probabilities in one forward pass (~33ms); open-weight (Apache 2.0), self-hostable alternative to TypeSafe AI's closed Jev API, with a Jev-compatible HTTP endpoint; trained via RLCD so honest probability reporting is reward-maximizing
- [Laya (Notification Center — aayushch)](entries/laya-notification-center.md) — AI-powered notification command center (Tauri + Svelte + Python) — unrelated to the convaiinnovations/laya decision model; aggregates notifications from multiple sources with AI-assisted triage and prioritization; desktop app
- [Leantime](entries/leantime.md) — Open-source project management system designed for non-project managers; combines strategy, planning, and execution with neurodivergence-friendly UX (ADHD, dyslexia, autism); self-hosted PHP/MySQL with Docker support, AGPLv3
- [LiteLLM](entries/litellm.md) — Open-source Python SDK and self-hosted AI gateway (proxy server) providing a unified OpenAI-compatible interface to 100+ LLM providers — model routing, automatic fallbacks, cost tracking, per-team budgets, Redis caching, guardrails, MCP/A2A support; ~40K stars, 1300+ contributors, YC-backed; MIT
- [LiteParse](entries/liteparse.md) — Open-source, local-first document parser from the LlamaIndex team (`@llamaindex/liteparse`, PyPI `liteparse`, crates.io `liteparse`) — PDFium-based native text extraction in ~2-5ms/page when a document already has selectable text, bundled Tesseract OCR fallback (or a pluggable HTTP OCR backend) for scanned pages, and a per-page `is-complex`/needs_ocr check for routing decisions; Rust core with Node.js, Python, and WASM bindings plus a `lit` CLI; ~12.9k stars.
- [LLaMA Factory](entries/llamafactory.md) — Unified fine-tuning framework for 100+ LLMs and VLMs — zero-code web UI (LLaMA Board) and CLI; supports SFT, LoRA/QLoRA (2–8 bit), DPO/KTO/ORPO/PPO, reward modeling, pretraining; multimodal (image/video/audio); vLLM/SGLang inference; NVIDIA/AMD/Ascend NPU/CPU; used by Amazon, NVIDIA, Aliyun; ACL 2024; Apache 2.0
- [llama.cpp Adaptive KV Streaming](entries/llama-cpp-adaptive-kv-streaming.md) — llama.cpp fork that streams KV cache pages from pinned host RAM to GPU on demand — runs Qwen3.8-27B at full 256K context on 16GB VRAM (RTX 5070 Ti) without UVM thrashing; phase arena multiplexes prefill/decode workspaces, adaptive policy adjusts resident/ring split in real time, V2 adds MTP (speculative decoding) with shared ring; CUDA-only, experimental, MIT
- [LlamaIndex](entries/llama-index.md) — Open-source Python data framework for building LLM/RAG and document-agent apps: data connectors, indices/graphs, retrievers, and query engines to augment LLMs with private data, with 300+ integration packages (LLMs, embeddings, vector stores) on LlamaHub. Pairs with the commercial LlamaParse/Parse agentic-OCR platform. MIT.
- [LlamaStash](entries/llamastash.md) — Terminal-native manager and daemon for local LLM runtimes — TUI + CLI, routes llama.cpp (GGUF) or any declared OpenAI-compatible server behind one proxy endpoint (default http://127.0.0.1:11435/v1), auto-discovers models cached by Hugging Face/Ollama/LM Studio, and patches Pi/OpenCode/Claude Code configs to route through it; MIT, Rust, single core maintainer (Deepu K Sasidharan)
- [LLM Compressor](entries/llm-compressor.md) — Transformers-compatible model quantization/compression library for optimized vLLM deployment — GPTQ, AWQ, SmoothQuant, AutoRound, rotation-based (SpinQuant, QuIP), and REAP expert pruning; weight/activation/KV-cache/attention quantization (INT8, FP8, NVFP4, MXFP4, MXFP8); saves in compressed-tensors format; DDP and disk offloading for large-model compression; maintained by Red Hat AI and the vLLM Project
- [LLM Wiki (nashsu)](entries/nashsu-llm-wiki.md) — Cross-platform Tauri desktop app implementing Karpathy's LLM Wiki pattern — ingests documents (PDF/DOCX/PPTX/XLSX/EPUB/images/web clips) and incrementally compiles them into a persistent, interlinked wiki instead of re-deriving answers via RAG each query; ships a knowledge-graph visualizer (sigma.js/graphology, 4-signal relevance, Louvain community detection), optional LanceDB vector search, a tool-using chat agent, a local token-protected HTTP API plus MCP server for Claude Code/Codex, and an Obsidian-compatible vault output; ~21k GitHub stars; GPLv3
- [LM Studio](entries/lm-studio.md) — Desktop application for downloading, managing, and running local LLMs with GUI chat, OpenAI/Anthropic-compatible API server (localhost:1234), MLX and llama.cpp engines, built-in model search with VRAM fit estimation, GPU offload controls, voice transcription, and Bionic agentic assistant; JS/Python SDKs, MCP client support, headless daemon (llmster), LM Link encrypted remote inference; free for personal and commercial use; macOS/Windows/Linux
- [ln](entries/ln.md) — Michael Fogleman's Go library/renderer that draws 3D scenes as vector line art instead of raster images — paths are chopped to a resolution, each point ray-cast toward the camera to test occlusion, and visible paths projected to 2D and output as PNG or SVG; supports primitives (sphere, cube, triangle, cylinder), OBJ/STL mesh import, CSG (union/intersection/difference), and custom vector-based texturing; built for pen-plotter output; last pushed 2019-07-19; MIT.
- [LocalAI](entries/localai.md) — Open-source local AI engine — drop-in OpenAI/Anthropic/ElevenLabs API compatibility across 60+ backends (llama.cpp, vLLM, whisper.cpp, stable-diffusion, MLX, etc.); supports LLMs, vision, voice, image, video on any hardware (NVIDIA/AMD/Intel/Apple/Vulkan/CPU); multi-user auth with quotas, built-in agents with MCP/RAG/tool use, distributed cluster mode, fine-tuning UI, WebRTC realtime audio; MIT
- [Locally Uncensored](entries/locally-uncensored.md) — All-in-one free, open-source local AI desktop studio (Windows/Linux, AGPL-3.0-only) combining chat, image generation, video generation, and a coding agent — no Docker, terminal, or cloud required; coding agent shows the diff before applying changes; supports local image models (Juggernaut XL V9, DreamShaper XL Turbo V2, FLUX.1, FLUX 2 Klein 4B, Z-Image Turbo, Qwen-Image 2.1); beta features include on-device voice conversation and gated computer-control; optional remote access via passcode-gated encrypted Cloudflare tunnel; maintained by GitHub user PurpleDoubleD with near-weekly releases (~1,750 stars as of Sept 2026); Windows installers currently ship unsigned
- [LocateAnything](entries/locate-anything.md) — novel parallel box decoding for vision-language grounding; 3B model on HuggingFace; evaluate for spatial/histology image analysis when pipeline matures
- [Manim (3b1b)](entries/manim.md) — Grant Sanderson's (3Blue1Brown) Python animation engine for explanatory math videos — scenes are Python classes composing Mobjects (LaTeX, shapes, graphs, 3D surfaces) with animations rendered via OpenGL, including an interactive live-preview mode; this is the original personal version, distinct from the community-maintained fork ManimCE; MIT, ~94k stars
- [MarkovJunior](entries/markov-junior.md) — Probabilistic programming language where programs are ordered lists of rewrite rules and inference is constraint propagation — generates dungeons, mazes, architecture, puzzles, and simulations on 2D/3D grids; C#/.NET, 153 examples, supports WFC and ConvChain nodes; MIT
- [Maxun](entries/maxun.md) — Capable no-code web data platform with MCP support and LLM extraction, but early-stage and AGPLv3 copyleft limits commercial embedding
- [Meetily](entries/meetily.md) — Privacy-first, self-hosted AI meeting assistant (Zackriya Solutions) that captures, transcribes, and summarizes meetings entirely on the local machine. Tauri desktop app (Rust backend, Next.js frontend) with live Whisper/Parakeet transcription, mic+system audio capture, and summaries via Ollama (local) or optional Claude/Groq/OpenRouter/OpenAI endpoints. MIT (Community Edition); a paid PRO tier exists.
- [Memoose](entries/memoose.md) — Local, no-API-key dual-path memory system for coding agents — SQLite knowledge graph, CLI + 26 MCP tools + skills/hooks harness; procedures stored as self-evolving 'procedural graphs' (steps as nodes, edges carrying conditions/advice/pitfalls) after Google's Procedural Graphs paper (arXiv 2609.09153)
- [MemPalace](entries/mempalace.md) — Local-first AI memory with 96.6% R@5 retrieval, 33 MCP tools, verbatim storage, and pluggable backends; strong benchmarks and Claude Code integration make it worth trialing
- [MemStack](entries/memstack.md) — Free-tier skill library is substantial but the PyPI MCP loader performs opaque license checks and stores credentials in-process, warranting supply-chain caution before adoption
- [Meridian](entries/meridian.md) — GPU-accelerated document processing pipeline — Docling (8 load-balanced instances) for PDF layout detection, Qwen3-VL via vLLM for table/figure/formula reading, Ollama for embeddings, Qdrant for vector storage; stateless Celery workers on CPU calling long-lived GPU services over HTTP; measured at 118 pages/min on a single H200; includes text-only path (pypdfium2, 1000 docs/min on CPU); Apache-2.0
- [Meshy](entries/meshy.md) — Commercial AI 3D-content platform — generates textured 3D models from text prompts ("Text to 3D") or images ("Image to 3D"/"Multi-Image to 3D"), plus AI texturing (PBR maps), auto-rigging, animation, remeshing/file conversion, and print-ready STL/3MF export; preview mesh in ~30s, textured model in under 2 minutes on the default Meshy 6 model, Meshy 7.1 branded for closer reference fidelity; bulk generation up to 50+ simultaneous tasks; access via web app, mobile app, REST API (official Python/Node.js SDKs), a Meshy MCP server, ComfyUI nodes, and Blender/Unity/Unreal plugins; for coding agents specifically, ships an open-source skill pack (`meshy-dev/meshy-3d-agent`, installed via `npx skills add meshy-dev/meshy-3d-agent`) with Claude Code/Cursor/OpenClaw skills that call the REST API directly, no MCP server required; closed-source hosted SaaS, Free (100 credits/mo, generated assets public) up to Pro $20/mo, Premium $40/mo, Ultra $100/mo, Studio from $60/mo, Enterprise custom; API-generated assets auto-delete after 3 days (non-Enterprise) per the API docs, though Meshy's own pricing page separately states 14 days for Studio — the two official pages disagree; SOC 2 Type 2, ISO 27001:2022, and GDPR compliance claimed.
- [Milvus](entries/milvus.md) — Go/C++ cloud-native vector database — fully distributed K8s-native architecture, HNSW/IVF/DiskANN/CAGRA GPU indexes, dense + sparse + BM25 full-text + hybrid search, lake-native data access (v3.0) with Apache Iceberg/Parquet/Lance, multi-tenancy with hot/cold storage, Milvus Lite for embedded use; LF AI and Data Foundation; Apache-2.0
- [MinerU](entries/mineru.md) — Open-source document parsing engine by OpenDataLab — converts PDFs, images, Office docs (Word/PPT/Excel), EPUB, OFD, HTML/MHTML, CSV into LLM-ready Markdown/JSON with four quality tiers (flash/basic/standard/advanced); VLM+OCR dual engine supporting 109 languages; local-first with optional remote API; CLI with agent-oriented continuation/locator system; local document library with search/watch/scan; MCP server; integrations with LangChain, RAGFlow, Dify, FastGPT; Python >=3.10; Apache-2.0 with commercial threshold (100M MAU or $20M revenue); ~80k stars
- [mirai-s-ada (Mirai S 27B on 12GB + agent layer)](entries/mirai-s-ada.md) — Serving recipe and launcher for Mirai Labs' Mirai S (Qwen3.8-27B-S, ~2.4-bit trellis GGUF) on a 12GB RTX 4070 — custom llama.cpp fork with 262K context, tiered q8_0 KV cache, and MTP speculative drafting at every depth (author-measured decode 70 / 61 / 20 / 10 tok/s at 4K / 64K / 128K / 200K context) — plus an optional OpenAI-chat-schema proxy 'layer' that adds a WASI-sandboxed `run_python` tool loop, auto-generated API cards for Python modules appended to the first user message, and an API check that flags nonexistent names/kwargs in earlier tool code; OpenAI-compatible API with bearer key; MIT
- [Mitsuba 3](entries/mitsuba3.md) — Research-oriented, retargetable forward/inverse renderer from EPFL — Python-first, JIT-compiled and differentiable via the Dr.Jit backend, supports monochromatic/RGB/spectral and polarized light transport, CPU and GPU execution; ~3k stars, 374 forks; BSD-3-Clause.
- [Mixpeek](entries/mixpeek.md) — Multimodal search/retrieval infrastructure ('multimodal data warehouse') running on object storage: typed feature-extraction pipelines turn video/files into queryable timestamped features (scenes, transcripts, faces, OCR, fingerprints), embeddings are generated/stored from 50+ models, and multi-stage retrievers compose filter/join/rerank (advertised <100ms). Integrations include S3, GCS, R2, Mux, LangChain, and MCP. Catalog source is a Mixpeek education video contrasting an 'extract once, upstream of every app' model against a plain vector database.
- [mlx-vlm TurboQuant (Apple Silicon KV Cache Compression)](entries/mlx-vlm-turboquant.md) — Production-quality Metal kernels implementing TurboQuant on Apple Silicon; benchmarked and ready for review but PR not yet merged
- [MotionBricks (NVIDIA)](entries/motionbricks.md) — NVIDIA's modular latent generative model (SIGGRAPH 2026 / ACM TOG) for real-time character and robot motion synthesis — given 4 recent poses and up to 4 target poses it generates the in-between motion at 30 Hz via a root-path module, a masked-token pose transformer, and a VQ-VAE decoder; chaining generations with application-supplied targets produces continuous locomotion, navigation, and object interaction ("Smart Primitives") with no retraining; ships pretrained ONNX checkpoints for the SOMA human body model and the Unitree G1 robot, a Python runtime (Warp/Newton/TensorRT) and demo/editor apps; requires an NVIDIA GPU (TensorRT); code Apache-2.0, models under the NVIDIA Open Model Agreement, bundled sample motions restricted to NVIDIA evaluation use only; ~561 stars, 55 forks.
- [Multi-Agent Custom Automation Engine Solution Accelerator](entries/microsoft-multi-agent-custom-automation-engine.md) — Microsoft solution accelerator where specialized AI agents plan, execute, and validate multi-step business tasks from natural-language input, built on Microsoft Agent Framework + Azure AI Foundry + Cosmos DB + Container Apps; ships reference scenario packs (product marketing, employee onboarding, retail remediation, RFP/contract review, content generation); MIT, explicitly marked proof-of-concept
- [Mycelium](entries/mycelium.md) — Self-hosted Node.js/SQLite coordination server for multi-agent teams — persistent agent identity, work queues, messaging, approval gates, context store, spend tracking, GPU drone queue, 79-tool MCP server, plugin system; runtime/LLM-agnostic (HTTP API + SDK + MCP); Apache-2.0
- [n8n](entries/n8n.md) — Fair-code workflow automation platform (TypeScript) — visual node-based editor plus custom JS/Python code, 400+ integrations, native AI/agent capabilities (LangChain-based AI nodes, MCP client and server support), triggers/webhooks/schedules; self-hostable via Docker or paid n8n Cloud; Sustainable Use License + n8n Enterprise License (fair-code, not OSI open source); ~204k stars
- [NavMeshPlus](entries/navmesh-plus.md) — Unity package extending Unity's NavMeshComponents with a 2D extensions system — auto-generates a navigation mesh from scene geometry (tilemaps, sprites, Collider2D) for top-down 2D pathfinding, letting characters and AI navigate around obstacles without hand-authored waypoints; 2.4k stars, 269 forks; MIT.
- [Neo4j](entries/neo4j.md) — Native property-graph database storing data as labeled nodes and typed relationships with key-value properties, queried via Cypher (now standardized as ISO GQL); open-core licensing — Community Edition (GPLv3) covers ACID transactions, native graph storage/indexing, vector and full-text indexes, and APOC's 450+ procedures, while Enterprise Edition (commercial license) adds role-based auth, LDAP/Kerberos, Causal Clustering, tens-of-billions-scale graphs, rolling upgrades, and hot backup; also offered as AuraDB managed cloud and a newer Infinigraph Edition with unlimited horizontal sharding; Graph Data Science library ships as a separate open-core component (Community: all algorithms, 4-CPU cap, 3-model catalog limit; Enterprise: unlimited cores, cluster write support).
- [nginx](entries/nginx.md) — HTTP web server, reverse proxy, content cache, load balancer, TCP/UDP proxy, and mail proxy — originally by Igor Sysoev, 2-clause BSD license; enterprise distributions and commercial support from F5; mature infrastructure for hosting agent services and API gateways
- [North Star](entries/north-star-osint.md) — Commercial SaaS OSINT platform unifying nine intelligence sectors — a live 250,000+ camera globe with local YOLOv8 detection, username/breach-database lookup, multi-chain crypto wallet tracing, network/port/.onion scanning, photo geolocation, radio-spectrum monitoring, trackable-link visitor telemetry (Fisherman), and 100+-registry public-records search — behind a paid subscription with no self-hosting or open-source code
- [NVIDIA Audio2Face-3D](entries/audio2face-3d.md) — NVIDIA technology collection that generates 3D facial animation — synchronized jaw/tongue/eye articulation plus emotional expression inferred from speech tone — from pre-recorded or real-time audio, driving a character via mesh deformation, joint transforms, or blendshape weights; ships as a C++/Python inference SDK (MIT), a Python+Docker model-training framework (Apache-2.0), an Autodesk Maya plugin (MIT), an Unreal Engine 5.4/5.5/5.6 Blueprint-integrated plugin with a MetaHuman sample project (MIT), and a gRPC containerized NIM microservice for multi-user deployment (NVIDIA Software License Agreement); pre-trained regression (v2.3) and diffusion (v3.0) lip-sync models (Mark/Claire/James voices) and emotion-inference models are distributed separately on Hugging Face under an NVIDIA Open Model license (Audio2Emotion models under a custom license restricted to use with Audio2Face only); mixed licensing across code and model-weight components.
- [NVIDIA Build (NIM)](entries/nvidia-build.md) — NVIDIA's model API catalog and NIM inference microservices; broad model access but enterprise-focused with GPU-heavy deployment requirements
- [NVIDIA Model Optimizer](entries/nvidia-model-optimizer.md) — NVIDIA's open-source library (formerly TensorRT Model Optimizer, rebranded Dec 2025) of LLM/VLM compression techniques — post-training quantization (NVFP4/FP8/INT8/INT4 via SmoothQuant, AWQ, SVDQuant), quantization-aware training/distillation, pruning, distillation, speculative-decoding draft training, and sparsity; takes Hugging Face/PyTorch/ONNX checkpoints, exports quantized checkpoints deployable on TensorRT-LLM, TensorRT, vLLM, or SGLang; publishes pre-quantized checkpoints (DeepSeek-R1-FP4, Llama-3.3-70B-FP4, Nemotron NVFP4) on Hugging Face; pip install nvidia-modelopt[all]; pre-1.0 (0.x), Apache-2.0.
- [NVIDIA NeMo Switchyard](entries/nemo-switchyard.md) — Model-routing layer that selects which LLM serves each request (efficient vs. capable) per call, embeddable as a library, run through a standalone OpenAI/Anthropic-compatible proxy, or loaded as a NeMo Relay / LiteLLM plugin
- [NVIDIA Nemotron 3 Ultra](entries/nemotron-3-ultra.md) — NVIDIA's most capable open model — 550B total / 55B active parameters, Mixture-of-Experts Hybrid Mamba-Attention architecture with LatentMoE and MTP speculative decoding; 1M context length; 5.9x throughput vs GLM-5.1, 4.8x vs Kimi K2.6; open checkpoints (base, post-trained, NVFP4 quantized, GenRM) and training datasets released
- [NVIDIA Nemotron 3.5 Bolt 74B-A9B (24GB)](entries/nemotron-3-5-bolt.md) — NVIDIA open-weight vision-language model for local agentic work — 74B total / 9B active Mamba-2–Transformer hybrid LatentMoE with C-RADIO v4-H vision encoder, fine-tuned from Nemotron 3.5 Super VL; natively 2-bit (IQ2) via quantization-aware training so the packed model fits in 24GB; text/image/video in, 256K context, thinking toggle via `enable_thinking`; releases: 24GB vLLM pack, 24GB GGUF (22GB + 1.73GB mmproj) for llama.cpp, BF16 for post-training, and a 1.1B LiLiCorr speculative-decoding drafter; OpenMDW-1.1 license; announced alongside NVIDIA PAIR
- [NVIDIA Personal AI Router (PAIR)](entries/nvidia-pair.md) — Open-source local inference router (public beta) that proxies AI app/agent requests to a single endpoint and schedules them across NVIDIA DGX Spark, RTX Windows/Linux PCs, and Apple Silicon Macs on the same LAN — infrastructure tool, not an agent/dev workflow component
- [OBLITERATUS](entries/obliteratus.md) — Mechanistic interpretability research toolkit for refusal removal — notable as a reference for alignment geometry, but not safe to adopt in agent pipelines and irrelevant to bioinformatics workflows
- [Odysseus](entries/odysseus.md) — Feature-rich self-hosted AI workspace with massive community momentum, but weeks-old with unsandboxed agent shell execution
- [Oh My Hermes (OMH)](entries/oh-my-hermes.md) — Operating layer for Hermes Agent — 101 installable workflow skills across six capability families (plan, build, research, code, create, operate) with deterministic routing, evidence boundaries, review-first project memory, and multi-language classification; Python-based, local-first control plane
- [OKF Agent Memory](entries/okf-agent-memory.md) — Git-native persistent memory framework for AI coding agents — a compact push-layer behavioral codex (AGENTS.md, ~100-150 tokens) enforced through Agent Action Grammar (AAG), plus a pull-layer knowledge bundle (OKF v0.2 Markdown+YAML) searched via a pure-Go embedded MCP server with sub-300us in-memory BM25; ships a linter enforcing AAG's token budget, ASCII-only syntax, and tool-signature parity rules
- [Ollama System One API (Decision Models)](entries/ollama-system-one-api.md) — Local decision-model endpoint in Ollama 0.35+ — Jev-compatible /v1/systemone API returns typed choices, yes/no probabilities, and rubric scores instead of generated text; ships three models (Nimble 9B from Bespoke Labs, Tev1 4B and 0.8B from Together AI); 91ms average latency on M5 Max; runs entirely offline; MIT
- [Omnigent](entries/omnigent.md) — Open-source 'meta-harness' giving a common orchestration layer over Claude Code, Codex, Cursor, OpenCode, Hermes, Pi, and custom YAML agents — swap/combine harnesses, mix vendors in one session, drive from terminal/browser/phone, run in cloud sandboxes (Modal/E2B/Daytona/etc.), and enforce approval/spend/tool policies; Python 3.12+, degraded native Windows mode
- [OmniRoute](entries/omniroute.md) — OmniRoute is a self-hosted AI gateway exposing a single OpenAI-compatible endpoint that routes requests across 271+ LLM providers using 18 routing strategies, an 11-engine token-compression pipeline, quota-sharing across API keys, and MCP/A2A protocol support
- [Onyx AI Platform](entries/onyx-ai-platform.md) — Full self-hostable AI platform with agentic RAG and MCP — notable architecture reference, but too deployment-heavy to adopt as a composable toolkit component
- [Open WebUI](entries/open-webui.md) — Leading self-hosted LLM web UI with RAG, pipelines, and enterprise auth — relevant infrastructure context but custom non-SPDX license limits adoption
- [OpenAI Codex Claude Code Proxy](entries/openai-codex-claude-code-proxy.md) — Two shell launchers that start Claude Code with GPT models as the main agent, routing through a local CLIProxyAPI instance that translates Anthropic Messages API requests to the OpenAI Responses API for OpenAI/Codex OAuth or Azure OpenAI, with per-tier model mapping for subagents
- [OpenAI Decisions API](entries/openai-decisions-api.md) — Hosted decision-model endpoint (POST /v1/decisions, model fixed to gpt-6-luna) that returns typed answers instead of free text — predicate (probability), choice (value + probabilities + confidence), and score (weighted score + probabilities + confidence) — from text, images, input_json, or conversation history; 1-400 questions per request; $0.10/1M uncached input tokens, no output-token charge, prompt caching (1,024-token minimum prefix); announced at DevDay 2026 (2026-09-29) in limited preview, now generally available with GPT-Live voice integration and Private Link; OpenAI claims up to 10x faster than a standard Responses API call to GPT-6 Luna, with a Box Extract case study reporting ~5x faster document routing at matching accuracy; supports Zero Data Retention, HIPAA, and regional data residency (US, EEA, Switzerland); competes with TypeSafe's Jev and Cloudflare's Clef/Clef-flash in the typed-decision-model space
- [OpenAI Python SDK](entries/openai-python-sdk.md) — Official Python client for the OpenAI API — GPT, o-series, Codex, DALL-E, Whisper, embeddings; typed Pydantic models, async support, streaming, function calling; MIT, PyPI
- [OpenClaw](entries/openclaw.md) — ecosystem signal for agentic workspaces; not a concrete kb comparison
- [OpenHands](entries/openhands.md) — OpenHands provides an Agent Canvas UI and Agent Server for running coding agents — its own built-in agent, Claude Code, Codex, Gemini, or any ACP-compatible agent — as a self-hosted, always-on engineering team with local, Docker, VM, or cloud backends
- [OpenMM](entries/openmm.md) — OpenMM runs molecular dynamics simulations as a standalone engine or embeddable library, with custom forces and integrators, CUDA/OpenCL GPU acceleration, AMBER/CHARMM/OpenFF/GAFF force fields, and support for arbitrary PyTorch models as ML/MM force terms
- [OpenRouter](entries/openrouter.md) — Unified LLM API gateway supporting 200+ models — useful as infrastructure reference, not directly needed when using Claude natively.
- [OpenSandbox](entries/opensandbox.md) — General-purpose sandbox runtime platform for AI applications (Alibaba-originated) — multi-language SDKs (Python, Java/Kotlin, JS/TS, C#/.NET, Go), unified sandbox lifecycle/execution APIs, Docker and Kubernetes runtimes, and an osb CLI plus MCP server for coding agents, GUI agents, agent evaluation, code execution, and RL training; Apache-2.0
- [OpenSpec Plus](entries/openspec-plus.md) — Spec-driven engineering workflow with strict TDD, adversarial review agents, and self-fixing on failures; similar approach to SpecShip
- [Orq.ai (Agent Runtime / LLMOps Platform)](entries/orq-ai.md) — Amsterdam-based (founded 2022, formerly Orquesta.cloud) proprietary enterprise control platform for the AI agent lifecycle — Agent Runtime sits in the request path to manage tools, memory, retries/handoffs, and multi-step orchestration; supports granular per-tool approval gates (an individual MCP tool can be flagged to pause and require human sign-off without gating the whole agent run), risk-based approval thresholds, a governed registry of approved agents/models/MCPs/skills/tools with pre-production approval and default-deny for anything outside it, plus guardrails, PII masking, RBAC, observability/tracing, and human-in-the-loop evaluation workflows; 300+ models across 17 providers via its AI Gateway; cloud/VPC/on-prem deployment with US/EU data residency, SOC 2 and GDPR compliance; closed-source SaaS, pricing not published
- [Outlines](entries/outlines.md) — Structured generation library by .txt — guarantees LLM outputs conform to JSON Schema, Pydantic models, regex, grammars, or Python types via constrained decoding (FSM-based token masking); works with transformers, llama.cpp, vLLM, Ollama, OpenAI, Gemini; Jinja prompt templates, function calling inference, batch generation; Rust core (outlines-core) for performance; 65M+ downloads; trusted by NVIDIA, Cohere, HuggingFace, vLLM; Apache-2.0
- [Overcut — Agentic SDLC Orchestration](entries/overcut.md) — Commercial orchestration layer for multi-agent SDLC; addresses real coordination gap but closed-source, early-stage, and not yet individually usable
- [Ovis-VL-Embedding](entries/ovis-vl-embedding.md) — Omni-modal embedding model (2B and 9B variants) built on Qwen3.5 backbone — encodes text, images, visual documents, and video frames in a shared representation space; 77.46 overall on MMEB-v2 (78 datasets), 80.47 on visual document retrieval; no modality-specific projection heads, last-token hidden state as embedding; for multimodal RAG, cross-modal search, document retrieval
- [OvisOCR2](entries/ovisocr2.md) — Compact 0.8B-parameter vision-language model for end-to-end page-level document parsing — given a document page image, generates a single structured Markdown document (plain text, LaTeX formulas, HTML tables, and visual regions in natural reading order) without a multi-stage OCR pipeline; scores 96.58 overall on OmniDocBench v1.6 and 75.06 Avg3 on PureDocBench per the publisher's technical report
- [PaddleOCR](entries/paddleocr.md) — PaddleOCR provides three product lines for document understanding — PP-OCRv6 scene text recognition, PP-StructureV3 document-structure-to-Markdown/JSON conversion, and the PaddleOCR-VL 0.9B-parameter vision-language model for full document parsing across 109 languages
- [PageIndex](entries/pageindex.md) — Vectorless reasoning-based RAG engine — replaces vector index with hierarchical tree index, uses LLM reasoning for retrieval instead of similarity search; 98.7% accuracy on FinanceBench (vs vector RAG baselines); SDK with local mode (pip install pageindex) and cloud mode; ~$0.001/page indexing cost; ideal for financial/legal/medical/technical documents; MIT
- [Pandera](entries/pandera.md) — Union.ai's dataframe validation library: define schemas (object-based DataFrameSchema or class-based DataFrameModel) with typed columns, built-in and custom checks, and validate pandas/polars/pyspark and other dataframe-like objects for correctness in data pipelines. MIT.
- [PaperBanana](entries/paperbanana.md) — Reference-driven multi-agent framework for automated academic illustration — five specialized agents (Retriever, Planner, Stylist, Visualizer, Critic) transform method descriptions and figure captions into publication-quality diagrams and plots via in-context learning, style guidelines, and iterative critic refinement; Gradio/Streamlit UIs, CLI, ClawHub skill; Google Research origin, Apache-2.0 (Google patents on core workflows restrict commercial use)
- [Paperless-ngx](entries/paperless-ngx.md) — Mature self-hosted document management system — useful for archiving research docs but not an agent tool or developer library
- [Parallax](entries/parallax.md) — Fully decentralized distributed LLM inference engine by Gradient — pipeline-parallel model sharding across heterogeneous devices (CUDA/Metal/CPU, mixed platforms, phones), P2P communication via Lattica, GPU backend via SGLang/vLLM, Mac backend via MLX LM, paged KV cache and continuous batching; supports DeepSeek, Qwen, GLM, Kimi-K2, gpt-oss, MiniMax; cross-platform
- [ParseBench](entries/parsebench.md) — LlamaIndex document parser benchmark — tests parsers across text, formatting, tables, charts, and page-location attribution; v1.0 with visual checks and scoring fixes (breaking change from v0.2)
- [PenEcho](entries/penecho.md) — Local-first AI canvas: put a question, equation, diagram, or half-formed idea anywhere on an infinite canvas and PenEcho reads the marks and their spatial relationships and answers beside them. Node server + browser UI; pluggable LLM source (Claude CLI, Codex CLI, Kimi CLI, or OpenAI/Anthropic API). A Kimi Open Source Friend. AGPL-3.0-only.
- [PentAGI](entries/pentagi.md) — Fully autonomous, multi-agent AI system (Go, MIT) for penetration testing — given a target and goal in plain English, a delegation system of specialized agents (research, development, infrastructure) plans and executes testing steps, running tools like Nmap and Metasploit inside an isolated Docker sandbox, and produces a report; supports OpenAI, Anthropic, Ollama, AWS Bedrock, Google AI/Gemini, DeepInfra, OpenRouter, DeepSeek, and Moonshot as LLM providers; ships a ctester utility for evaluating agent-model capability; self-described as an autonomous/assistant-guided platform, not a CALDERA-style BAS product with predefined attack campaigns
- [pgvector](entries/pgvector.md) — PostgreSQL extension for vector similarity search — HNSW and IVFFlat indexes, exact and approximate nearest neighbor, vector/halfvec/bit/sparsevec types, 6 distance functions (L2/IP/cosine/L1/Hamming/Jaccard), iterative index scans, binary quantization, multitenancy via partitioning, hybrid search with pg full-text; all Postgres guarantees (ACID, JOINs, WAL replication); PostgreSQL License
- [Photon Studio](entries/photon-studio.md) — Free proprietary desktop image editor (macOS/Windows 11/Linux) from Tenzen Studio positioned as a Photoshop alternative — layers, masks, smart objects, curves, liquify, local (on-device) background removal, and native PSD read/write; local-first with no upload or account required; reported by press as built largely via AI-assisted ('vibe-coded') development for roughly $2,000 in model tokens
- [Pi Coding Agent](entries/pi-coding-agent.md) — Minimal, extensible terminal coding agent harness by Mario Zechner (Earendil Works). Four core tools (read, write, edit, bash), ~200-token system prompt, 37+ models across 20+ providers (Anthropic, OpenAI, Google, Bedrock, etc.). Extensible via TypeScript extensions, skills, prompt templates, themes, and packages — deliberately omits MCP, sub-agents, plan mode, and permission popups in favor of user-built alternatives. Tree-structured sessions with branching, compaction, and forking. SDK/RPC modes for embedding. MIT. ~78k GitHub stars.
- [PinchTab](entries/pinchtab.md) — Standalone Go HTTP server (~16MB single binary) that gives AI agents control of Chrome via a CLI and HTTP API: token-efficient text extraction (~800 tokens/page), headless/headed profiles, multi-instance orchestration, an MCP server, and a dashboard. Local-first by default (binds 127.0.0.1, sensitive endpoints off). MIT.
- [Pinecone](entries/pinecone.md) — Fully managed, closed-source serverless vector database — proprietary Rust engine separates compute from storage, consumption-based pricing (~$0.33/GB-month storage plus metered read/write units), targets ~200ms query latency at 80% p95 recall; offers hosted embedding/reranking (Pinecone Inference), Pinecone Assistant, Dedicated Read Nodes, and Bring-Your-Own-Cloud for data residency; no self-hosting option.
- [Pixel Agents](entries/pixel-agents.md) — Animated pixel-art office that visualizes AI coding agents as characters — renders live activity (editing, reading, waiting for input) via Claude Code hooks or transcript heuristics; ships as VS Code extension and standalone CLI (npx pixel-agents); agent-agnostic HookProvider interface with Claude Code as reference integration; office layout editor, sub-agent/team visualization, areas mapped to workspace folders; React 19 + Canvas 2D; MIT
- [PixelRAG](entries/pixelrag.md) — Open-source RAG framework from Berkeley Sky Computing Lab/BAIR/Berkeley NLP that renders web pages, PDFs, and images to screenshot tiles and retrieves over the images directly (via a LoRA-fine-tuned Qwen3-VL-Embedding-2B model) instead of parsing text, preserving tables/charts/layout; supports text or image queries; ships a hosted API over a pre-built 8.28M-page Wikipedia index, a Claude Code plugin (pixelbrowse) that lets Claude screenshot and read a page instead of fetching raw HTML, FAISS or Qdrant backends, and a separate LoRA training pipeline; Apache-2.0
- [Piyaz](entries/piyaz.md) — Agentic workspace where people and AI coding agents collaborate on projects — context network with dependency-graph task management, 6 MCP tools, end-to-end composer orchestration (Claude Code), plugins for Codex/Cursor/Antigravity; hosted + self-hostable, AGPL-3.0
- [Podman](entries/podman.md) — Daemonless, rootless-capable container engine with a Docker-compatible CLI for managing OCI containers, pods, images, and volumes; uses Buildah for builds, Netavark/Aardvark for networking, and crun/runc as the OCI runtime; ships Podman Desktop as a GUI frontend
- [Polars](entries/polars.md) — Rust-core columnar DataFrame library with a multi-threaded, vectorized (SIMD) query engine and lazy query optimizer; Python/Rust/Node/R APIs, Apache Arrow memory model, streaming out-of-core execution, and readers/writers for CSV/JSON/Parquet/Delta/Arrow/databases/cloud storage. MIT.
- [PolyStrata](entries/polystrata.md) — Fork/extension of Strata that adds a second expert-offloading inference engine for more MoE models from ordinary GGUF files — GLM 5.3 Flash, Qwen3.6-35B-A3B, Ornith 1.5, Gemma 4 26B-A4B, Tiel Coder, DeepSeek V4 Flash, LFM2.5, MiniCPM-V and others — beside Strata's unchanged Qwen3.8-Flash-Next engine; GPU caches the most-used experts while the CPU computes the rest from the memory-mapped GGUF, plus draft-token speculation; one-click installer (Windows/Linux), web UI, OpenAI/Anthropic-compatible API on localhost:8080; NVIDIA RTX 20–50 (AMD HIP beta on Linux); author reports ~70–87 tok/s for Qwen3.6-35B-A3B on an 8GB RTX 4070 laptop; MIT
- [PrimeTween](entries/primetween.md) — Allocation-free Unity tweening library — animates Transform, RectTransform, SpriteRenderer, AudioSource, Light, Materials, UI Toolkit elements, and generic float/Color/Vector values in one line of code with sequences, delays, and coroutine/async-await support (delays allocate as part of C# async, nothing else does); tunable from the Inspector; pitched as a faster, allocation-free alternative to DOTween; a paid PRO tier adds visual Inspector-authored animation; free tier license is source-available but restricts repackaging/resale, not OSI-approved MIT/Apache; 2.0k stars, 83 forks.
- [Procedura](entries/procedura.md) — Agentic text-to-3D modeling pipeline — turns a text prompt into an editable parametric OpenSCAD program with named parts joined by typed mates; optional per-part PBR materials, articulation export to OpenUSD/URDF with Isaac Sim validation; part-by-part generation with LLM reasoning, 3D feedback loop, and refine critic; any OpenAI-compatible endpoint; MIT
- [PullMD](entries/pullmd.md) — Self-hosted anything-to-Markdown conversion service for AI agents — converts web pages (including Reddit/Hacker News threads with comment trees), PDFs, Office documents, EPUB, images, audio, and YouTube videos (transcripts with timecodes) into clean, boilerplate-stripped Markdown with metadata in YAML frontmatter; multi-strategy extraction (Cloudflare's Markdown endpoint plus parallel Readability/Trafilatura scored for quality); exposed via a PWA, REST API, MCP server, and a Claude Code skill; v3 added a markitdown document-conversion sidecar and an opt-in media tier for image captioning/audio transcription via any OpenAI-compatible endpoint; multi-arch Docker images; AGPL-3.0
- [PulsarForge](entries/pulsarforge.md) — CPU-only MoE inference engine in C11 — streams 744B-parameter models off SSD via expert-level LRU caching, cross-layer predictive prefetch (80.8% recall), and dynamic-k expert truncation; 21x speedup over naive on i7-8550U/32GB
- [PyScript](entries/pyscript.md) — Open-source platform for running Python in the browser via Pyodide (CPython on WASM) or MicroPython; embed Python in HTML with a <script type='py'/'mpy'> tag to build browser apps that run client-side on desktop, mobile, or any browser. Born at Anaconda; Apache-2.0.
- [Qdrant](entries/qdrant.md) — Rust-based vector similarity search engine and database — HNSW with inline payload filtering, dense/sparse/multi-vector search, hybrid search with RRF/DBSF fusion, TurboQuant 4-bit/8-bit compression, GPU-accelerated indexing, distributed sharding and replication, gRPC and REST APIs, Edge variant for in-process use; Apache-2.0
- [Qwen2.5-Coder-32B-Instruct](entries/qwen25-coder-32b.md) — Alibaba's 32.5B-parameter code-specialized open-weight LLM — 64-layer transformer with GQA (40Q/8KV), 128K context via YaRN, trained on 5.5T tokens including source code and synthetic data; coding abilities reported as matching GPT-4o; Apache-2.0
- [Qwen3.6-27B](entries/qwen3-6-27b.md) — Strong open-weight 27B model with near-frontier coding scores and 262K context; promising local worker model but requires multi-GPU or quantization for practical use
- [Qwen3.8-27B-Uncensored-HauhauCS-Aggressive-MTP-GGUF](entries/qwen3-8-27b-uncensored-hauhaucs-aggressive-gguf.md) — Community-produced GGUF quantization of Qwen3.8-27B with safety-refusal behavior deliberately stripped ('uncensored'/abliterated 'Aggressive' profile, publisher-claimed 0/465 refusals), published by pseudonymous Hugging Face contributor HauhauCS; ships multiple quant levels plus a vision projector and a custom 'FastMTP' speculative-decoding sidecar requiring a llama.cpp patch
- [RAG-Anything](entries/rag-anything.md) — All-in-one multimodal RAG framework from HKU's Data Intelligence Lab, built on LightRAG — parses PDFs/Office/images/audio/video via MinerU, Docling, or PaddleOCR, then builds a multimodal knowledge graph linking text, tables, equations, and images; three query modes (text, VLM-enhanced, direct multimodal); 23,500+ GitHub stars; MIT
- [Redis Iris](entries/redis-iris.md) — Redis's context-and-memory platform for AI agents (announced May 18, 2026) — bundles real-time structured/unstructured data retrieval (Context Retriever), short- and long-term agent memory (Agent Memory: interaction history, user preferences, persistent attributes), and continuous data sync from source-of-record systems (Redis Data Integration) into one product, layered on existing Redis Search and LangCache semantic caching
- [Redpanda](entries/redpanda.md) — Kafka-compatible data streaming platform in C++ — single-binary deployment (broker + schema registry + HTTP proxy + Raft consensus), 10x lower latency than Kafka, 3-6x cost efficiency, no JVM/ZooKeeper dependency; Redpanda Connect ships 300+ connectors including AI connectors; tiered storage and cloud topics; BSL 1.1 (source-available)
- [REINVENT 4](entries/reinvent4.md) — REINVENT 4 generates optimized small molecules matching a user-defined multi-component property profile via reinforcement learning over RNN and transformer generative models, supporting de novo design, scaffold hopping, R-group replacement, linker design, and library design
- [Release Manager Assistant (RMA)](entries/microsoft-release-manager-assistant.md) — Microsoft solution accelerator that augments release managers with a multi-agent system (Planner, JIRA Agent, Azure DevOps Agent via MCP, Visualization Agent, Fallback Agent) for cross-system release planning, scheduling, and readiness reporting; built on Azure AI Foundry + Microsoft Agent Framework + Redis; ships mock/synthetic-data MCP servers for JIRA and Azure DevOps so it can be evaluated without live system access; MIT
- [Repowire](entries/repowire.md) — Compelling multi-agent mesh for cross-repo coordination, but macOS/Linux only and default spawn config ships dangerous permission flags
- [repowise](entries/repowise.md) — Local-first codebase-intelligence engine: index a repo once and keep it current per-commit, then serve it to coding agents via ~11 task-shaped MCP tools plus a CLI, local dashboard, VS Code extension, and a zero-LLM PR bot. Builds a five-layer index (dependency graph across 19 languages, git behavioral signals, generated wiki, mined architectural decisions, deterministic 1–10 code-health scores with concrete refactoring plans), plus change-risk scoring, dead-code detection, and reversible command-output 'distill'. Structural index needs no API key and runs offline; AGPL-3.0 with a commercial license option.
- [Robyn](entries/robyn.md) — Async Python web framework with a Rust runtime (PyO3); sync/async handlers, dynamic routing, middleware, WebSockets, built-in OpenAPI generation, Jinja2 templating, hot reload, CLI scaffolding, plus built-in AI-agent routing and an MCP server mode. No external server needed. BSD-licensed.
- [RustDesk](entries/rustdesk.md) — Open-source remote desktop application (Rust core, Flutter UI) designed for self-hosting as a TeamViewer/AnyDesk alternative — end-to-end encrypted P2P with relay fallback, cross-platform (Windows/macOS/Linux incl. Wayland, iOS, Android, web), self-hostable rendezvous/relay server (hbbs/hbbr) so no traffic touches vendor infrastructure; AGPL-3.0, ~123k stars
- [RuView WiFi Sensing Platform](entries/ruview-wifi-sensing.md) — Interesting hardware+agent integration with a Claude Code plugin and MCP server, but beta software requiring physical ESP32 hardware for core capabilities
- [salt.md](entries/salt-md.md) — Self-hosted open-source workspace for people and AI agents — single static Go binary with embedded frontend, SQLite storage, and a built-in MCP server so Claude, Codex, Cursor, Gemini CLI, ChatGPT or any MCP client read and write the same pages and databases under the same per-person permissions; block editor, typed collections (select/date/person/relation/rollup/formula) with table/board/list/gallery/calendar/timeline/form views, full-text search, realtime co-editing, comments, activity log, graph view; macOS/Linux desktop shell; used for task pages where agents leave 'why' notes so another agent can pick up the work; AGPL-3.0
- [SAM 3 (Segment Anything Model 3)](entries/sam3.md) — Meta's 848M-param unified foundation model for promptable segmentation in images and video; adds open-vocabulary detection/segmentation of all instances of a concept from a short text phrase or visual exemplars, on top of SAM 2's point/box/mask tracking. Checkpoints are gated (access request + HF auth).
