- [SANA-WM (NVIDIA)](entries/sana-wm.md) — 2.6B-parameter open-source text+image-to-video diffusion transformer world model from NVIDIA/MIT (Haoyi Zhu, Song Han, Enze Xie, et al.) — given one image, a text prompt, and a 6-DoF camera trajectory, generates an explorable, controllable, minute-long 720p video on a single GPU via Hybrid Linear Attention (Gated DeltaNet + periodic softmax attention) and an LTX-2 refiner; Apache-2.0 code and weights, trained in 15 days on 64 H100 GPUs
- [Save Game Free](entries/save-game-free.md) — Bayat Games' free Unity save/load library — serializes game data to Binary, JSON, or XML with optional encryption via a static SaveGame.Save/Load API, supports serializable complex/custom classes, works across Windows/Linux/macOS/Android/iOS/tvOS/Samsung TV/WebGL, and persists state across scene loads without reinitialization; a paid Save Game Pro tier adds support for saving native Unity types (GameObjects, Transforms) directly; 672 stars, 65 forks; MIT.
- [ScaleDown Constrained Decoding Models (CDM)](entries/scaledown-cdm.md) — Hosted product line of task-specific 'Constrained Decoding Models' from ScaleDown — Classify, Extract, Summarize, and a pre-existing fourth model Compress — that make one prefill pass over up to 1M tokens of multimodal input and pick an answer/label/span directly from it instead of generating text token-by-token; Classify returns a calibrated probability + reason per label, Extract returns JSON with confidence scores and source citations, Summarize returns a consistent summary under 1,000 tokens; launched Oct 13, 2026, positioned against TypeSafe's Jev, Cloudflare's Clef, and OpenAI's Decisions API in the typed-decision-model space; claims 200x faster and 467x cheaper than frontier LLM APIs and 77.2% vs Jev's 72% on a decision benchmark (self-reported); $0.05/million input tokens (OpenRouter lists $0.04), output free; hosted API only in public docs (api.scaledown.xyz, x-api-key auth), though ScaleDown's CTO has separately described an undocumented self-hosted deployment option; LangChain integration via langchain-scaledown middleware, also listed on OpenRouter and Vercel
- [ScaNN](entries/scann.md) — Google Research library for efficient large-scale vector similarity search — anisotropic vector quantization (AVQ) for Maximum Inner Product Search plus SOAR indexing, partition/score/rescore search pipeline, native Python and optional TensorFlow APIs, AVX/NEON-optimized x86/ARM wheels; powers Vertex AI Vector Search; Apache-2.0
- [ScrapeGraphAI](entries/scrapegraph-ai.md) — Python web-scraping library that uses LLMs plus graph-based pipelines (SmartScraperGraph, SearchGraph, SpeechGraph, ScriptCreatorGraph, and multi-page variants) to extract structured data from websites and local documents (HTML, XML, JSON, Markdown); MIT-licensed open-source core with a separate paid managed cloud API/SDKs
- [Screenshot to Code](entries/screenshot-to-code.md) — Converts screenshots, mockups, Figma designs, and screen recordings into HTML+Tailwind, HTML+CSS, React+Tailwind, Vue+Tailwind, Bootstrap, or Ionic+Tailwind code via multi-provider LLMs (OpenAI/Anthropic/Gemini); Gemini extracts real logos/images from the source rather than regenerating them, Replicate handles image generation/editing/background removal; React/Vite frontend + FastAPI backend, self-hostable or hosted at screenshottocode.com; MIT, 55k+ stars
- [sdf](entries/sdf.md) — Michael Fogleman's Python library for building 3D (and 2D) models from signed distance functions — primitives (sphere, box, torus, cylinder, cone, Platonic solids) composed with boolean ops (union, difference, intersection, including smooth/blended variants), transforms (translate, rotate, scale, twist, bend), and repetition (linear/circular arrays); meshes via Marching Cubes and exports STL natively plus 20+ formats (OBJ, PLY, ...) via meshio; also renders 2D SDFs from text/images for extrusion; actively maintained (pushed 2026-09-30); MIT.
- [SearXNG](entries/searxng.md) — Free, self-hostable, privacy-first federated metasearch engine (fork of the discontinued searX) — queries dozens of external search engines/services in parallel and aggregates results without tracking users, storing queries, profiling, or ads; supports !bang shortcuts to target a single engine and extensive per-engine/ranking customization; no official hosted/cloud offering, self-hosting (typically Docker) is the only deployment model though public community instances exist; ~15k GitHub stars
- [SGLang](entries/sglang.md) — High-performance LLM/VLM serving framework from UC Berkeley/LMSYS — RadixAttention automatically reuses KV cache across requests with shared prefixes via radix tree, achieving 75–95% cache hit rates on multi-turn agent workloads; structured generation language for constrained decoding; powers 400K+ GPUs in production (xAI, NVIDIA, AMD, LinkedIn); part of PyTorch ecosystem; Apache-2.0
- [Shannon (AI Pentester)](entries/shannon.md) — Autonomous white-box AI pentester that analyzes source code and executes real exploits against web apps/APIs; AGPL-3.0, proof-by-exploitation reports, but actively mutates target state and requires authorization context
- [Shepherd (shepherd-agents/shepherd)](entries/shepherd-agents.md) — Python runtime substrate that records agent runs as durable, inspectable, reversible execution traces — agent-written outputs land as retained proposals (changesets) that a human selects, applies, releases, or discards, with OS-level (Seatbelt/Landlock) enforcement of per-task read/write permission grants declared in function signatures
- [SIRA (Superintelligent Retrieval Agent)](entries/sira.md) — Meta (FAIR) research pipeline that uses LLMs to enrich both documents and queries to improve BM25 retrieval without training. Five stages: data prep, BM25 indexing, corpus enrichment (LLM indexing phrases), query expansion (LLM search terms), and LLM pointwise reranking; reports SOTA on BEIR using only inference-time compute. MIT.
- [smolagents](entries/smolagents.md) — HuggingFace's minimal code-first agent framework — CodeAgent writes actions as Python (30% fewer steps than JSON tool calling), sandboxed execution via E2B/Blaxel/Docker, model/modality/tool-agnostic, CLI included; Apache-2.0, 26K stars, 207 contributors
- [SonarQube](entries/sonarqube.md) — Self-hosted continuous-inspection platform for code quality and security — static analysis (symbolic execution, data/control-flow analysis) across 20+ languages via quality gates and 6,000+ rules; free Community Build (LGPL-3.0) lacks branch/PR analysis and advanced security (taint analysis, secrets detection, IaC); paid Developer/Enterprise/Data Center editions add those plus portfolios and enterprise reporting; free IDE companion SonarLint gives real-time in-editor feedback; priced per instance/year by lines of code
- [Soup](entries/soup.md) — LLM fine-tuning CLI — one YAML config, one command; supports SFT, DPO, GRPO, PPO, KTO, ORPO, SimPO, IPO, BCO, pre-training, distillation, vision/audio; layer streaming trains an 8B model on a 4 GB GPU by streaming frozen base from host RAM one decoder layer at a time (bit-exact); auto batch size, GPU detection, quantization (4-bit/8-bit/FP8/QAT); backends: transformers, Unsloth, MLX; web UI dashboard; export to GGUF/ONNX/TensorRT/AWQ; 100+ model recipes; Python 3.10-3.12; Apache-2.0
- [SpacetimeDB](entries/spacetimedb.md) — Relational database that is also the application server (Clockwork Labs, Rust) — schema + business logic compile into a WASM module running inside the database, clients connect directly and subscribe to tables with automatic real-time state sync; ACID with in-memory state plus commit log; modules in Rust/C#/TypeScript/C++, client SDKs for web/Unity/Unreal; powers the BitCraft Online MMORPG backend; BUSL-1.1 (→AGPL-3.0 with linking exception), ~25k stars
- [sparse-memory-lm (product-key memory on a 21M model)](entries/sparse-memory-lm.md) — Hobby research repo testing product-key memory layers (Lample et al. 2019; Meta 'Memory Layers at Scale') on a small model — a 21M-parameter Llama-style decoder plus a learned table of up to 16.8M rows × 384 dims (6.4B values, ~384 rows / 33M values read per token) matches a ~114M dense model's perplexity on 500M Wikipedia tokens; 4-bit table memory-mapped from NVMe generates ~114–140 tok/s using 0.4GB VRAM (prompt prefill slow from page faults); portable Triton kernels verified on RX 9070, MI350X, H100/H200; bolting a table onto pretrained Qwen3.5-0.8B did not help; Apache-2.0
- [SpecShip](entries/specship.md) — AWS spec-driven autonomous engineering framework (Kiro Power) — brownfield recon, contract-first markdown specs, strict TDD gates, adversarial parallel validation with up to 7 subagents, and self-healing fix agents
- [Spiderbrain V3](entries/spiderbrain-v3.md) — BSL license; master/column concept is the key takeaway for kb graph
- [SPlisHSPlasH](entries/splishsplash.md) — Open-source C++ library (Jan Bender et al.) for physically-based fluid simulation using smoothed particle hydrodynamics (SPH) — 2D/3D, CPU/GPU neighborhood search, AVX vectorization, Python bindings, multiple pressure solvers (WCSPH, PCISPH, PBF, IISPH, DFSPH, PF), viscosity/surface-tension/vorticity methods, multi-phase and rigid-fluid coupling; 1.9k stars, 339 forks; MIT.
- [SQLTools](entries/sqltools.md) — VS Code extension for database management — connects to MySQL, PostgreSQL, SQL Server, SQLite, CockroachDB, MariaDB, TiDB via official drivers, plus 25+ community drivers (DuckDB, BigQuery, Snowflake, Oracle, ClickHouse, etc.); SQL formatter/beautifier, query runner with history and bookmarks, connection explorer, INSERT query generator; pluggable driver architecture; maintained by George James Software; MIT
- [StarNet](entries/starnet.md) — Local-first, MIT-licensed multi-agent harness (Tauri/Rust desktop shell + Node.js sidecar) for Windows/macOS that runs several AI agents in parallel with a live pixel-art visualization of agent state, delegation (team.dispatch), chat integration (Telegram, Discord, Slack, Signal, Matrix), persistent memory, BYOK model backends (OpenRouter, Anthropic, OpenAI, Google, Ollama, Claude Code CLI), and per-job/per-day spend budgets enforced against a real cost ledger
- [Storybloq](entries/storybloq.md) — PolyForm Noncommercial license + heavy overlap with kb wiki
- [Strands Agents SDK](entries/strands-agents.md) — Open-source Python/TypeScript SDK for building and running AI agents in-process — agent loop with lifecycle controls, tools, MCP, multi-agent patterns, memory/sessions, guardrails, tracing, and evals; defaults to Amazon Bedrock with Anthropic, OpenAI, Gemini, and Ollama provider support
- [Strata](entries/strata.md) — One-click inference engine for Qwen3.8-Flash-Next (125B MoE) on consumer NVIDIA GPUs (12-24GB) — expert offloading between GPU/RAM/SSD, speculative decoding via built-in MTP, 60-95 t/s on RTX 5070; multi-GPU pipeline, OpenAI/Anthropic-compatible API, vision support, browser UI with live monitor; built on ggml; MIT
- [Subtext](entries/subtext.md) — Live Jacobian-lens viewer that renders a local model's internal 'global workspace' during chat — reads Anthropic's Jacobian lens at 9 layers per token over Qwen3.5-4B, showing verdicts/plans forming before they are spoken; browser canvas + per-token ledger, scrubbable GPU-free replays; validated against Anthropic's reference impl; Apache-2.0
- [Supabase](entries/supabase.md) — Open-source Postgres development platform (Firebase alternative) with built-in auth, storage, realtime, edge functions, REST/GraphQL APIs, and pgvector AI toolkit — massive community (36K+ commits), self-hostable, Apache-2.0
- [Swift 1.5 Qwen3.8-27B (UkisAI)](entries/swift-1-5-qwen3-8-27b.md) — UkisAI's reasoning-efficient post-trained derivative of Qwen3.8-27B — reduces thinking tokens by a publisher-reported 58.5% while scoring 0.35% higher than the base model on aggregate benchmarks (GPQA-Diamond, C-Eval, IFBench, ERQA, AIME 2026, HMMT, LiveCodeBench v6, Terminal-Bench 2.1), with savings holding across xhigh/medium/low reasoning-effort settings; ships BF16, GGUF/GSQ-RCO GGUF, AWQ/AutoRound/GPTQ INT4, NVFP4, AMD Quark FP8, and Apple MLX exports plus a free OpenAI-compatible demo API; Swift Open License v1.0 (free under $1M annual revenue, enterprise license required above)
- [Synapse (memory-layer)](entries/synapse-memory-layer.md) — Early-stage, self-hosted persistent memory backend for LLMs (Claude Desktop, Claude Code/VS Code via MCP) that stores structured 'memory atoms' plus immutable evidence 'signals' in Postgres+pgvector behind a write-quality/critic/risk-gate commit pipeline; roadmap includes routing low-confidence questions to specific human experts and folding their answers back into memory.
- [Taiga-S1](entries/taiga-s1.md) — 1.2M-parameter System-1 decision model for building CAD parts in FreeCAD — ~1ms per decision on CPU, no LLM/GPU/screenshots; covers PartDesign workflows (sketch, pad, pocket, hole, revolve, pattern, fillet, chamfer, shell); generalizes to 17-feature parts (95%) despite training on ≤5-feature; 86-100% recovery from 20% random action corruption; part of the Biome-S1 family; 4.9 MB model file
- [TencentDB Agent Memory](entries/tencentdb-agent-memory.md) — Tencent-built team-level memory hub for AI agents providing four asset types — layered Chat Memory (L0-L3), versioned Skills, linked Wiki pages, and a CodeGraph — managed through a human-reviewable Memory Hub panel with ACL-based sharing
- [Textual](entries/textual.md) — Textualize's Python TUI framework for building cross-platform terminal user interfaces with a simple, optionally-async API and a large widget library (buttons, trees, data tables, inputs, text areas); apps can also be served to a web browser via textual serve / Textual Web. MIT.
- [Third Brain V5 (Wiki/Knowledge Layer)](entries/third-brain-v5-wiki.md) — Obsidian-specific schema, heavy; staleness and contradiction detection concepts worth noting for kb
- [Titans (Atlas + Cronus)](entries/titans-atlas-cronus.md) — Local-first, agent-first infrastructure pair from a solo AI-assisted builder — Atlas is persistent project memory (knowledge packages, typed graph, evidence/provenance, SQL, blobs, audit history; retrieval blends lexical + vector + graph + evidence, scoped per project/tenant), Cronus is durable execution (DAG workflows, scheduling, checkpoints, at-least-once retries with lease fencing, dead-letter, approval gates); shared MCP server over stdio plus REST/gRPC; free binaries, proprietary source, signed releases (SHA-256 + Ed25519); Windows/Linux
- [tkinter](entries/tkinter.md) — Python's standard library interface to the Tcl/Tk GUI toolkit, bundled with CPython on Windows, macOS, and Linux
- [Tolvi — Engineering Decision Vault](entries/tolvi.md) — Solid CAG/RAG dual-mode decision-capture design but pre-1.0, small project, and overlaps with kb already in use
- [torchtune](entries/torchtune.md) — PyTorch-native post-training library by Meta — composable building blocks (not trainers) for SFT, LoRA, QLoRA, DPO, GRPO, quantization, evaluation; YAML-driven recipes under 600 LOC; composable parallelism (FSDP2, TP, SP, CP, expert parallel for MoE); in-backward optimizer fusion; works on single 24GB GPU to multi-node clusters; v0.6 stable; Apache 2.0
- [TRL (Transformers Reinforcement Learning)](entries/trl.md) — Hugging Face full-stack post-training library — SFT, GRPO, DPO, reward modeling, PPO, and more; vLLM integration for online RL; Harbor sandboxed agent training; vision-language model alignment; co-located vLLM for GPU efficiency; integrated with transformers/PEFT/DeepSpeed/Liger Kernel; Apache 2.0
- [turbopuffer](entries/turbopuffer.md) — Serverless vector and full-text search database built object-storage-first (S3/GCS/Azure Blob as source of truth, ~$0.02/GB) with an NVMe SSD and RAM caching hierarchy layered on top — cold namespaces cost near-zero, hot namespaces get sub-10ms p50 cached-query latency; write-ahead log on object storage for durability (~10K+ vectors/sec writes), strong consistency by default; no free tier, $64/month minimum; proprietary, no self-hosted version; production users include Cursor (95% cost reduction after migrating from Pinecone), Anthropic, Notion, Linear.
- [Twine](entries/twine.md) — Open-source tool (since 2009, by Chris Klimas) for writing choice-based, state-tracking interactive fiction as linked "passages" shown as boxes on a map, connected by [[double-bracket]] links that become both player choices and map arrows; variables and if-checks gate content on player choices (e.g. a trust variable). Ships as a desktop/browser/Electron app (klembot/twinejs, GPL-3.0, ~2.9k stars); default story format is Harlowe (Leon Arnott, Zlib license), with SugarCube, Snowman, and Chapbook as alternatives. "Publish to File" exports a single self-contained HTML file playable in any browser or uploadable to itch.io; "Export as Twee" produces a plain-text passage format a separate parser can read into another engine. Netflix's "Black Mirror" episode Bandersnatch was written in Twine. Current release 2.12.1 (2026-10-06).
- [Ultimate DeepSeek UltraCode (Reasonix Fleet)](entries/ultimate-deepseek-ultracode.md) — Claude Code extension routing Workflow/UltraCode fan-out lanes to DeepSeek v4-flash via a local Reasonix gateway — keeps Claude as main agent, runs subagent work on cheaper DeepSeek inference with ephemeral sessions and server-side cache optimization reaching 99%+ hit rates
- [Ultralytics YOLO](entries/ultralytics-yolo.md) — Unified real-time computer vision framework — object detection, instance/semantic segmentation, classification, pose estimation, oriented bounding boxes, and tracking; YOLO26 (latest) is NMS-free, DFL-free, 43% faster CPU inference than YOLO11; five model sizes (Nano to X-Large); CLI + Python API; export to ONNX/TensorRT/CoreML/TFLite; AGPL-3.0 (Enterprise license available)
- [UniTask](entries/unitask.md) — Cysharp's allocation-free async/await library for Unity — a struct-based UniTask<T> (vs. the heap-allocating Task<T>) that makes Unity's AsyncOperations, Coroutines, and MonoBehaviour/uGUI events awaitable, runs on Unity's PlayerLoop without threads (works on WebGL/wasm), and adds async LINQ (Channel, AsyncReactiveProperty) plus a TaskTracker window for leak detection; commonly used to replace coroutines for timers and sequencing (e.g. day/night cycles); requires Unity 2018.3+ for C# 7.0 task-like builders; 11.3k stars, 1.0k forks; MIT.
- [Unsloth](entries/unsloth.md) — LLM fine-tuning and inference framework — trains 500+ models up to 2x faster with 70% less VRAM via custom Triton kernels; Unsloth Studio web UI for running/training text, audio, vision, embedding models; supports LoRA, full fine-tune, RL (GRPO), FP8, pretraining; GGUF/safetensors export; NVIDIA/AMD/Intel/Apple Silicon; API endpoint for Claude Code/Codex integration; Apache 2.0 (core) + AGPL-3.0 (Studio UI)
- [Unstructured](entries/unstructured.md) — Open-source document ETL library — partitions 60+ file types (PDFs, Word, HTML, images, emails) into structured elements (Title, NarrativeText, Table, etc.) with layout ML enrichment, OCR, chunking, and embedding; connectors for 40+ sources/destinations; MCP server (Unstructured Transform) for agent-driven document processing; Apache-2.0, 15k+ stars
- [Vaultwarden](entries/vaultwarden.md) — Production-grade self-hosted Bitwarden-compatible password server in Rust; fraction of the resource footprint of the official server
- [Vespa](entries/vespa.md) — Open-source (Apache-2.0) big-data serving engine from Yahoo (open-sourced 2017, spun out as Vespa.ai in 2023) combining hybrid search (BM25 keyword + vector + tensor operations), real-time indexing, and machine-learned multi-phase ranking at billions-of-documents scale with sub-100ms latency; stateless container clusters handle query/indexing logic separately from stateful content clusters holding the data; available self-hosted or as Vespa Cloud; used in production by Spotify, Perplexity, Etsy, and others.
- [vLLM](entries/vllm.md) — High-throughput, memory-efficient open-source inference and serving engine for LLMs — PagedAttention KV-cache management, continuous batching, prefix caching, speculative decoding, and tensor/pipeline/data/expert/context parallelism; supports 200+ Hugging Face model architectures; originated at UC Berkeley's Sky Computing Lab, now a PyTorch Foundation hosted project (joined Dec 2024, hosted since May 2025); ~96k GitHub stars, 2000+ contributors; Apache-2.0
- [VoiceStudio](entries/voicestudio.md) — Open-source local-first ElevenLabs alternative — voice cloning, voice design, video dubbing, dictation, transcription, and audiobook creation in 646 languages; Electron desktop app with local API and MCP server for agent integration; NVIDIA CUDA and Apple Metal acceleration; AGPL-3.0
- [WaveFunctionCollapse](entries/wave-function-collapse.md) — C# algorithm (by Maxim Gumin) that generates bitmaps/tilemaps locally similar to a single input example — picks the lowest-entropy region, collapses it to one of the input's NxN patterns weighted by their input frequency, and propagates that constraint outward, repeating until the whole output is determined or a contradiction forces a retry; used for procedural terrain, dungeon, and texture generation; 25.5k stars, 1.4k forks; MIT (sample images/tiles excluded from the license).
- [Weaviate](entries/weaviate.md) — Go-based AI-native vector database — stores objects with vectors, integrated vectorization via OpenAI/Cohere/HuggingFace modules, semantic + BM25 keyword + hybrid search, built-in RAG and reranking, ACORN filtered search, multi-tenancy with RBAC, object TTL, gRPC/REST/GraphQL APIs; BSD-3-Clause
- [webosbrew (Homebrew Channel for LG webOS)](entries/webosbrew.md) — Community hub for rooting LG webOS Smart TVs — hosts the Homebrew Channel (a sideloaded on-device app store/manager unlocking apps like Kodi and Moonlight, ambient-light integrations such as HyperHDR, screensaver/wallpaper changes and remote-button remapping once installed), a directory of current software root exploits tracked per webOS version and patch date (faultmanager, DejaVuln, WTA, crashd, RootMy.TV, GetMeNow, plus a hardware DEBUG-flag method), and a model/firmware compatibility checker (CanI.RootMy.TV).
- [Weft](entries/weft-language.md) — Novel AI orchestration language with typed nodes, durable execution, and human-in-the-loop primitives — early-stage but architecturally interesting
- [WhisperX](entries/whisperx.md) — Whisper + wav2vec2 forced alignment + pyannote speaker diarization — 70x realtime batched transcription with accurate word-level timestamps and speaker IDs; builds on faster-whisper; ideal for lecture/interview transcripts
- [Yarn Spinner](entries/yarn-spinner.md) — Open-source dialogue system (Secret Lab) for writing and running branching game dialogue in a screenplay-like scripting language — the Yarn Spinner compiler/runtime is engine-agnostic, with an official Unity integration (YarnSpinner-Unity) sending lines, player-choice options, and in-scene commands from scripted conversations to the game; used in shipped titles including Night in the Woods, A Short Hike, and Dredge; Unity integration repo: 845 stars, 129 forks; MIT.
- [Zed](entries/zed.md) — Rust code editor (from the team behind Atom and Tree-sitter) with a native Agent Panel for agentic AI workflows — built-in agent reads/writes/runs code in a project; external coding agents (Claude, Codex, OpenCode, Copilot, Cursor, Gemini CLI, Qwen Code) connect via the Agent Client Protocol (ACP), with Zed hosting the thread UI while the external agent owns its runtime; inline edit predictions and multiplayer collaboration; editor core open-source, paid tiers sell Zed-hosted AI and admin controls; ~90k GitHub stars

### hook

- [opus-fable-mode](entries/opus-fable-mode.md) — Data-driven behavioral governor + re-injection hook to steer Opus 4.8 toward Fable 5's terse, result-first working style; honest about limits (style not capability) and backed by 37K-message corpus analysis

### mcp-server

- [agent-link](entries/agent-link.md) — End-to-end encrypted channel between coding agents on different machines: the agents split work, hand off tasks, and report progress to each other. Transport is a shared private git repo (an orphan 'agent-link' branch) — no server, account, or open ports — with an optional relay for sub-second delivery. Installs an MCP server (link_* tools) plus a notification hook for Claude Code and/or Codex, and a human CLI. Frames are AES-256-GCM sealed and device-signed. Python 3.10+, one dependency, Windows/Linux/macOS.
- [API Anything](entries/api-anything.md) — MCP server, CLI, and TypeScript library that turns a website's own frontend requests into a reusable HTTP "operation" an agent can call instead of driving a browser — a headless browser loads the target page once per example input, the part of the request that varies becomes a parameter, and cookies/tokens become references to a local store rather than literal values. Calls replay as plain HTTP where possible, falling back to in-browser fetch or full page render; when a learned operation drifts, the first caller to hit the failure repairs it and the fix is shared to a public Cloudflare Worker catalog (30 bundled read operations across 14 sites — Google Flights, Goodreads, Rotten Tomatoes, Hacker News, X, Instagram, YouTube, Steam, Airbnb, Amazon, Rightmove, Pinterest, LinkedIn, Domino's Australia). Writes are opt-in (--allow-writes) and guarded (write-interception during learning, duplicate-write refusal, idempotency keys). MIT, TypeScript, ~1.7k GitHub stars / 179 forks as of October 2026 (per trendshift.io), 2 contributors.
- [Claude Peers MCP](entries/claude-peers-mcp.md) — Novel multi-session peer messaging, but mandatory --dangerously-skip-permissions flag is a hard blocker for safe use
- [CodeGraph](entries/codegraph.md) — Pre-indexed code knowledge graph via MCP — tree-sitter extracts symbols/edges into SQLite, single codegraph_explore tool returns verbatim source + call paths + blast radius in one call; auto-syncs on file changes via native OS watcher; 34 languages, framework route detection (17 frameworks), cross-language bridging (Swift↔ObjC, React Native, Expo); 100% local, MIT
- [Docling MCP](entries/docling-mcp.md) — MCP server for document conversion, processing, and generation via the Docling library — converts PDFs and other documents into structured DoclingDocument JSON with OCR, table structure detection, and image handling; supports local, remote (Docling Serve), and hybrid modes; RAG integrations with Milvus, LlamaIndex, and LlamaStack; LF AI & Data project from IBM Research Zurich, MIT, 730+ stars
- [Godot MCP (Coding-Solo)](entries/godot-mcp.md) — MCP server (npm package @coding-solo/godot-mcp) that lets an AI agent launch the Godot editor, run a project in debug mode, capture debug/error output, list projects in a directory, query the installed Godot version, and manage UIDs for Godot 4.4+; installs via `claude mcp add godot -- npx @coding-solo/godot-mcp`; the most-forked and most-cited server under the generic 'Godot MCP' name per third-party MCP-server directories, ahead of numerous same-named community forks and rewrites that add scene-tree editing, screenshot capture, and C# support; MIT.
- [Granola MCP](entries/granola-mcp.md) — MCP server from Granola that gives AI tools (Claude, ChatGPT, Cursor, Claude Code) access to meeting notes — search conversations, pull context, create follow-ups from what was discussed; remote server at mcp.granola.ai/mcp; requires paid Granola plan
- [MCP Server YouTube Transcript](entries/mcp-server-youtube-transcript.md) — MCP server exposing a single get_transcript tool for YouTube videos — language fallback, optional timestamps, ad-strip filtering, zero transcript-fetch dependencies; fills the YouTube-to-markdown gap for learning-vault capture
- [n8n MCP Server](entries/n8n-mcp.md) — powerful workflow automation bridge but heavy dependency; evaluate when pipeline orchestration needs outgrow kb dispatch
- [OLS MCP Server](entries/ols-mcp-server.md) — MCP server for EBI Ontology Lookup Service — search terms, explore hierarchies, find similar terms across GO, HPO, MONDO, ChEBI, UBERON and all EBI-hosted ontologies; directly addresses LLM ontology hallucination problem
- [pdf-mcp](entries/pdf-mcp.md) — MCP server for surgical PDF access by AI agents — 13 tools for hybrid search (BM25 + semantic), selective page reading, table/image/chart extraction, OCR via Tesseract, corpus-wide multi-document search, and multi-column/CJK layout support; SQLite cache persists across restarts. MIT.
- [Perplexity MCP Server](entries/perplexity-mcp-server.md) — Official Perplexity MCP server providing real-time web search, conversational AI, and advanced reasoning tools to MCP-compatible clients; one-click install for Claude Code, Cursor, Codex; npm package @perplexity-ai/mcp-server; requires Perplexity API key
- [Prompt Caching MCP](entries/prompt-caching-mcp.md) — An MCP server that helps developers building Anthropic SDK applications optimize prompt caching by automatically placing cache_control breakpoints on stable content blocks, with tools to analyze cacheability and track cumulative token savings
- [REA (Reverse Engineer Anything)](entries/rea.md) — Open-source MCP server and CLI (npm package rea-agents) that connects coding agents (Claude Code, Codex, Cursor, Gemini CLI, Grok Build, and others) to reverse-engineering tooling — native binaries, JavaScript/Electron apps, .NET assemblies, Android APKs, websites, firmware, packet captures, and dozens of other artifact types — so an agent can inspect a target's behavior without its source and return pseudocode/evidence the agent uses to explain a feature or reimplement it. Setup (`npx rea-agents setup`) registers the MCP server and workflow instructions for the chosen agent(s), writing to `~/.claude.json` and copying skill files into `~/.claude/skills` (applies to future projects, not just the current one). Deep native-binary analysis needs a caller-supplied Hopper, Ghidra, IDA, or Kuna installation (setup can install Hopper with approval); static JS/.NET/APK inspection needs no native engine. Requires Node.js 22.23.4+/24.22.1+/26.12.1+ and npm; many per-target features need their own external tool (JADX+JDK, Apktool, adb, tshark, sigrok-cli, pwntools, mitmdump, WABT). Repo created 2026-04-14, single primary maintainer (1,566 commits as of review), MIT license for REA-authored code (third-party dependencies and commercial decompilers like IDA/Hopper/JEB keep their own licenses and require the user's own authorization). Went viral in October 2026 —
- [Skill Search MCP](entries/skill-search-mcp.md) — Semantic skill retrieval MCP for Claude Code — replaces per-turn skill listing tax (~7.3K tokens) with vector search over descriptions; saves 3.19% of 200K context window per turn at 117 skills, recall@3 of 0.79; MIT, service-free default
- [Slack MCP Server (korotovsky)](entries/slack-mcp-server-korotovsky.md) — Feature-rich MCP server for Slack workspaces — 18 tools covering channel history, thread replies, message search, unread messages, DMs/group DMs, message posting, reactions, saved items, user search, user groups; stealth mode (no bot install required), OAuth mode, Enterprise/GovSlack support; Go, Stdio/SSE/HTTP transports; MIT
- [Synthesize Bio](entries/synthesize-bio.md) — Generates synthetic human gene expression profiles on demand via natural-language prompts — unique bioinformatics capability, but proprietary SaaS requiring account and external API trust.
- [Unusual Whales MCP](entries/unusual-whales-mcp.md) — Official Unusual Whales MCP server — 100+ market data endpoints covering options flow, dark pool, congressional trading, Greek exposure, volatility, earnings, ETFs, shorts, fundamentals, and technicals; 30+ analysis prompts; remote or local mode; MIT

### meta-skill

- [One Skill to Rule Them All](entries/one-skill-to-rule-them-all.md) — proposes NEW skills from observed patterns; complementary to superpowers routing
- [OpenSpace](entries/openspace.md) — Compelling skill-evolution engine for Claude Code agents but very new (v0.1.0), self-published benchmark, and significant security surface from community skill ingestion
- [Skill Router](entries/skill-router.md) — superpowers using-superpowers already handles skill routing
- [SkillForge](entries/skillforge.md) — Evidence-driven skill creator for Claude Code/Codex — RED/GREEN baseline testing (fresh agent without skill vs. with skill), cross-runtime dedup and compilation, per-skill regression evals, ecosystem doctor for trigger collisions/duplicates, opt-in friction mining from session history, proactive advisor via hooks; MIT
- [SkillOpt](entries/skillopt.md) — Microsoft research framework that optimizes agent skills via training loops; +23.5 point lifts on Claude Code, Sleep mode for nightly skill consolidation is directly relevant

### plugin

- [/brag — Launch Video Generator](entries/brag.md) — Claude Code skill/plugin that generates short shareable launch videos from any project — analyzes the codebase, creates a product story with music/motion/share copy, renders via Hyperframes; one command; works with Claude Code, Codex CLI, opencode, Gemini CLI, Cursor via skills CLI; optional AI voiceover via Kokoro; MIT
- [Agent Skills (Addy Osmani)](entries/agent-skills-osmani.md) — Production-grade 24-skill engineering pack for AI coding agents — full SDLC lifecycle (define/plan/build/verify/review/ship) with anti-rationalization tables, verification gates, and 8 slash commands; 65K+ stars, Claude Code marketplace-installable, multi-platform (Cursor/Gemini/Windsurf/Copilot/Kiro); MIT
- [Andrej Karpathy Skills (multica-ai)](entries/andrej-karpathy-skills.md) — CLAUDE.md + Cursor rules implementing 4 of Karpathy's coding principles (Think Before Coding, Simplicity First, Surgical Changes, Goal-Driven Execution); Claude Code marketplace plugin + .cursor/rules/.mdc format; overlaps with karpathy-12-rules (covers 4 of 12)
- [Atomic Claude](entries/atomic-claude.md) — Opinionated Claude Code configuration bundle that replaces per-session repo re-discovery with a standing local code graph and wiki: a tree-sitter symbol graph (31 languages, 23 web frameworks, no compiler) indexes functions/types/imports/calls plus SQL lineage (Postgres/MySQL/T-SQL/Snowflake, dbt models) into a local SQLite file, answering callers/call-sites/blast-radius as file:line instead of grep text matches; layers an /autopilot plan→implement→review→ship loop (fresh-context implementer/reviewer split, tests-first, blast-radius check before edits), a self-maintaining repo/realm wiki, a self-sharpening config (/retrospective-learning), and an inter-session message bus. Free, fully local — no server, no account, nothing uploaded; ships as one static binary. MIT.
- [awesome-gamedev-agent-skills](entries/awesome-gamedev-agent-skills.md) — A library of 77 Agent Skills for game development spanning 12 engines (Godot, Unity, Unreal, Ebitengine, Phaser, PixiJS, three.js, Bevy, pygame, LOVE, Defold, Roblox), plus cross-engine discipline, genre-template, and workflow skills, installed via a router that detects the project's engine from project files and loads only the matching skills
- [BlitzOS](entries/blitzos.md) — Context-repo system for Claude Code cloud VMs — generates a private GitHub repo (CLAUDE.md map + git submodule references + sessions log) so every new cloud agent boots knowing your repos, conventions, and what previous agents did; one-click multi-repo launch via blitzos.com, self-hostable on Cloudflare; MIT
- [Builder Skills (BuilderIO/skills)](entries/builderio-skills.md) — Builder.io's pack of 15 composable agent skills (visual-plan, visual-recap, webmcp, agent-watchdog, plan-arbiter, efficient-fable, rewind, quick-recap, etc.) installable via npx @agent-native/skills across Claude Code, Codex, Cursor, Pi, OpenCode, and Copilot; doubles as a Claude Code / Cowork plugin marketplace; MIT, ~4.2k stars
- [Cartographer](entries/cartographer.md) — Claude Code plugin plus companion Bun CLI for codebase mapping/documentation: the plugin's legacy skill orchestrates parallel Sonnet subagents into docs/CODEBASE_MAP.md and a CLAUDE.md summary, while the v2 CLI indexes a repo into a local SQLite graph for bounded agent briefs, removal/completeness audits, and evidence-backed notes
- [Claude Autoresearch (uditgoenka)](entries/claude-autoresearch.md) — Claude Code / OpenCode / Codex skill+command pack generalizing Karpathy's autoresearch loop (constraint + mechanical metric + autonomous iteration + git rollback) to any domain — 14 commands (autoresearch, plan, debug, fix, security, ship, scenario, predict, learn, reason, probe, improve, evals, regression), a v2.2 orchestrator that derives a success predicate from a plain-language goal, and 9 always-on safety hooks; MIT
- [Claude Code Game Studios](entries/claude-code-game-studios.md) — Claude Code template that turns one session into a 49-agent, 74-skill simulated game studio with a three-tier director/lead/specialist hierarchy, 15 validation hooks, 14 path-scoped coding rules, and 62 document templates; ships engine-specific agent sets for Godot 4, Unity, and Unreal Engine 5; a project.yaml modes.rigor setting (minimal/standard/full) trades process weight for speed — the project's own measurement built four games from one brief and found the heavier standard-rigor build produced 30 process documents and lost a blind playtest ranking to the minimal-rigor build, which produced only a one-page brief; agents ask before acting and the user approves every write by default; MIT license.
- [Claude Code Setup Plugin](entries/claude-code-setup-plugin.md) — Official Anthropic plugin that scans a codebase and recommends tailored Claude Code automations — top 1-2 recommendations per category (MCP servers, skills, hooks, subagents, slash commands); read-only analysis, no file modifications
- [Claude for Financial Services](entries/anthropic-financial-services.md) — Official Anthropic reference agents and skills for financial services — 10 named workflow agents (Pitch Agent, Market Researcher, GL Reconciler, etc.), 7 vertical skill bundles (IB, equity research, PE, wealth management, fund admin, ops), 12 MCP data connectors (FactSet, S&P, Morningstar, PitchBook, LSEG, etc.); dual deployment as Cowork plugins or Managed Agents API; Apache-2.0
- [Claude SEO](entries/claude-seo.md) — Open-source SEO analysis plugin for Claude Code — 25 sub-skills and 18 specialist agents running in parallel across technical SEO, E-E-A-T content quality, Schema.org markup, AI search optimization (GEO), local SEO, e-commerce, and international SEO; falsifiable recommendations with leading indicators; 4-tier Google API integration; 8 optional MCP extensions (Ahrefs, DataForSEO, SE Ranking, etc.); MIT
- [Claude Skills (Rezvani)](entries/claude-skills-rezvani.md) — 355-skill library for 13 AI coding tools — 18 domains (engineering, marketing, product, compliance, C-level advisory, research, finance, business ops), 602 stdlib-only Python CLI tools, 711 reference templates; cross-platform (Claude Code, Codex, Gemini CLI, Cursor, Aider, Windsurf, and 7 more); MIT
- [Codex Plugin for Claude Code](entries/codex-plugin-cc.md) — Official OpenAI plugin enabling Codex usage from within Claude Code — code review (/codex:review), adversarial review, task delegation (/codex:rescue), session transfer, and background job management; requires ChatGPT subscription or OpenAI API key
- [dotclaude](entries/dotclaude.md) — Turnkey .claude/ folder setup — 7 specialist agents (code/security/perf/doc reviewers, silent-failure-hunter, frontend-designer), 12 workflow skills (/ship, /pr-review, /tdd, /debug-fix, etc.), 6 modular rules, 8 safety/productivity hooks; marketplace-installable, evidence-based setup scan; MIT
- [Engram (Learning Engine)](entries/engram-learning-engine.md) — Evidence-based Claude Code learning plugin — FSRS spaced repetition, free-recall verification with blind assessor grading, first-principles concept graphs, interactive HTML explorables; three commands (/learn, /review, /coach); Python 3 stdlib only, local JSON storage
- [Everything Claude Code (ECC)](entries/ecc-plugin.md) — rate-limited (429 errors); trial when accessible
- [Fable Agent Orchestration](entries/fable-agent-orchestration.md) — Apache-2.0 skill database for multi-agent orchestration — 24 reusable skills covering worktree-isolated parallel PR workflows, two-critic review loops, agent PR validation against real diffs/CI, fake-green test detection, orphaned WIP recovery, instruction-drift control, behavior-contract harnesses, and phase-aware engineering ladders
- [Fast Jev Compaction](entries/fast-jev-compaction.md) — Claude Code plugin/npm library that replaces the built-in compaction summary with a Jev (TypeSafe AI) scored prune of tool calls/results; depends on Claude Code's unshipped function-hooks feature and an early-access third-party API, and a filed issue shows its hook events go unrecognized (silent no-op) on a recent Claude Code build
- [FluidDocs Deck Builder](entries/fluiddocs-deck-builder.md) — Well-built skill/plugin pack for HTML slide decks; interesting multi-reviewer architecture but outside bioinformatics workflows
- [Frontier](entries/frontier.md) — Well-architected multi-harness orchestration runtime for Claude Code; early preview but the token-economics argument and delegation model are sound
- [Hackingtool Plugin](entries/hackingtool-plugin.md) — Claude Code plugin wrapping 183 pentesting/OSINT tools from Z4nzu/hackingtool — auto-selects backend (native Bash, WSL, Docker with purpose-built images), 56 plug-and-play / 127 environment-dependent tools across 20+ categories; MIT
- [Headcount](entries/headcount.md) — Claude Code plugin structured as a 16-department company org chart — 146 skills across CEO, Technology, Security (reviewer-class), IT Ops, Product, Marketing, Demand Gen, Revenue, Finance, Operations, PMO, Customer Experience, Data & Analytics, Corporate Strategy, People/HR, and Legal & Risk (reviewer-class); each department independently installable via marketplace, skills addressed as department:skill, agent charters for subagent delegation, exclusive write-surface enforcement; MIT
- [Headroom Desktop](entries/headroom-desktop.md) — Solid local-first Claude Code cost optimizer with hook injection into ~/.claude/settings.json — worth monitoring given agent-lockdown relevance, but macOS-only stable and single-contributor
- [ICCA Harness](entries/icca-harness.md) — Evidence-gated maker-checker-auditor workflow for Claude Code — separates Implementer, Checker, Control, and Auditor roles so the agent that writes code never decides whether it's accepted; mechanical enforcement via Git hooks (frozen acceptance tests, content-fingerprinted commit gates, bounded repair loops); measured 28% reduction in expensive-model tokens on implementation-heavy tasks at identical success rate (total tokens increase 37%); optional stage gates for static analysis, coverage, mutation testing, property tests, dependency structure, e2e scenarios; MIT
- [Jev-kit](entries/jev-kit.md) — Multi-component Claude Code integration kit wiring TypeSafe's Jev (a ~0.3s typed-decision model) into the agent loop: a PreToolUse tool-call guard ('Airlock', code rules first + one Jev question for the ambiguous ~7%), sub-agent tier right-sizing, a 'Belay' check that sends unverified 'done' claims back to the agent, plocate/Everything-based file search, a Jev-driven browser agent (vendored fork of browser-use's jev-ultrafast), plus installer/doctor/warm-daemon tooling; MIT, requires a TypeSafe API key, Linux/WSL2-first with a reduced-feature native Windows port
- [keepwarm (Claude Code prompt-cache keep-warm mod)](entries/keepwarm.md) — Claude Code mod (early-access plugin/hooks feature) that keeps an idle interactive session's prompt cache warm by forking a tool-less, text-free copy of the session's own transcript as a cache-read ping once 57 minutes pass with no model request — restarts the 1-hour cache TTL without adding anything to the conversation; stops after 20h since the last real prompt, after a refresh that reads under 1,000 cached tokens, or if a refresh gets no reply; turns itself off for a session if a refresh has to write more of the conversation than it reads (cache unreachable — 6 of 132 refreshes on record did this); never runs under claude -p or the SDK. Controlled via /keepwarm on|off|status and env vars (KEEPWARM_OFF, KEEPWARM_IDLE_MIN, KEEPWARM_TTL_MIN, KEEPWARM_MAX_HOURS, KEEPWARM_PROMPT); state kept locally in ~/.claude/state/keepwarm/<session id>.json. Installed by cloning into a folder referenced by CLAUDE_CODE_PLUGIN_DIRS.
- [llm-wiki (nvk)](entries/nvk-llm-wiki.md) — Claude Code plugin (with ports for Codex, DeepSeek Harness, OpenCode, and generic SKILL.md agents) implementing Karpathy's LLM Wiki pattern — treats raw sources as immutable "source code" and the LLM as a "compiler" that synthesizes them into a structured, source-linked wiki; organizes topics as isolated sub-wikis in a hub, runs parallel multi-agent research (5/8/10 agents per depth mode), and enforces that no wiki page exists without a cited source; commands include /wiki init, /wiki:research, /wiki:thesis, /wiki:query, /wiki:ingest, /wiki:compile; ~1.5k GitHub stars; MIT
- [Medley](entries/medley.md) — Y Combinator-backed Claude Code/Codex plugin providing /mission command for multi-agent orchestration — decomposes goals into coordinated plans across multiple models (Claude, GPT, Gemini, Grok, Qwen, DeepSeek, Ollama), with browser-based mission board for visibility
- [Microsoft Eval Guide](entries/microsoft-eval-guide.md) — A Claude Code plugin providing six skills that walk through a five-stage evaluation lifecycle (discover, plan, generate & baseline, run, interpret & improve) for AI agents built on Microsoft Copilot Studio, with architecture-aware scoping and interactive HTML dashboards
- [opencode-fff-search](entries/opencode-fff-search.md) — Drop-in OpenCode grep/glob replacement with Rust-backed in-memory index, frecency ranking, and SIMD literal matching — 3-10x faster than process-spawning ripgrep at scale.
- [pi-system-one](entries/pi-system-one.md) — Pi coding-agent extension registering a single system_one tool for bounded decisions (choice, noul, score) with calibrated probabilities; a thin wrapper over the provider-neutral system-one-core SDK, so any compatible 'System One' backend (TypeSafe Jev, Reflex, custom /v1/systemone endpoints) can sit behind it; pi-bifrost (same author) is a separate consumer of the same SDK
- [Ponytail](entries/ponytail.md) — YAGNI enforcement plugin with strong benchmarks (80-94% less code, 47-77% cheaper); multi-platform support and clean design make it worth trialing
- [pstack (Cursor Plugin)](entries/pstack.md) — Cursor plugin by Lauren Tan (poteto, React core team) packaging her own engineering discipline as agent workflows: 23 task playbooks (bug fix, perf, feature, refactor, investigation, shipping, autopilot-full/stack, etc.) routed by a single /poteto-mode entry point, 24 one-rule-each engineering principles (prove-it-works, fix-root-causes, boundary-discipline, guard-the-context-window, etc.) indexed inline at task start, situational sub-skills (/architect, /blast-radius, /interrogate, /arena, /swarm, /unslop, /no-comments, /tdd, /correct, /create-verification-skill and others), and two subagent types (poteto-agent, Comment Sicko). Explicit goal is less code at higher quality, not throughput. Installs as a Cursor or Claude Code plugin (/plugin install pstack@cursor-plugins); MIT; several community ports exist for Codex, Pi, OpenCode, and other hosts.
- [Scientific Agent Skills (K-Dense)](entries/scientific-agent-skills.md) — Scientific Agent Skills is a library of 163 curated Agent Skills that turn AI coding agents into scientific research assistants, spanning bioinformatics/genomics, cheminformatics/drug discovery, clinical research, ML/AI, scientific databases, and lab automation, each scanned with the Cisco AI Defense Skill Scanner
- [Second Brain Skills](entries/second-brain-skills.md) — Claude Code skill collection for knowledge work — MCP client with progressive disclosure, PPTX/carousel generator with brand system, SOP/runbook creator, Remotion video creator, skill creator guide, and brand & voice generator; by Cole Medin
- [Suede Creator Skills](entries/suede-creator-skills.md) — MIT-licensed pack of 25 Claude Code / Codex skills covering multi-agent orchestration (WIP collision detection, rollback trees), Codex worker fleets (Claude plans + parallel Codex CLI workers), A-F code review grading across 7 lanes with instant-F triggers, AI eval framework, design/copy/SEO audits, and consumer automation; ships MCP server with 7 tools
- [Thermos (Thermo-Nuclear Review, Cursor Plugin)](entries/thermos.md) — Cursor plugin running two parallel diff-scoped review subagents and synthesizing deduped findings: thermo-nuclear-review (deep branch audit for bugs, breakages, security, devex, feature-gate leaks) and thermo-nuclear-code-quality-review (strict maintainability audit — 'code judo' restructurings, a 1,000-line file limit, spaghetti-conditional growth, architectural-boundary leaks). Supersedes the thermo-nuclear-code-quality-review skill previously shipped standalone in Cursor's cursor-team-kit plugin. MIT; installs via /add-plugin thermos.
- [Understand Anything](entries/understand-anything.md) — Interactive knowledge graph plugin for codebases with multi-agent pipeline and broad platform support; impressive but heavy — watch for maturity and real-world performance on large repos
- [UnrealClaude](entries/unrealclaude.md) — Unreal Engine 5.7 editor plugin that docks a Claude Code chat panel in the editor and shells out to the local `claude` CLI (no separate API key) for in-editor assistance, plus a bundled Node.js MCP bridge exposing 20+ tools for actor manipulation, Blueprint/Animation-Blueprint editing, level management, materials, and Enhanced Input; by default the plugin invokes `claude -p --skip-permissions` for every prompt, removing Claude Code's human-in-the-loop approval step unless the integrator changes AllowedTools in C++; a separate 'Auto-approve script execution' project setting (default OFF) controls whether Python/C++/console scripts triggered through the bridge or chat run without a per-script permission dialog; hidden MCP tools (execute_script, run_console_command) can reach the Python interpreter if un-hidden by editing source; Windows/Linux/macOS (Apple Silicon); MIT; built from source, no prebuilt binaries.

### reference

- [5 AI Engineering Projects to Build](entries/ai-engineering-project-ideas.md) — Curated list of 5 hands-on AI engineering projects — LLM cost autopilot, semantic caching layer, model regression detector, failure forensics tool, self-healing technical docs; sourced from Instagram reel
- [A Global Workspace in Language Models](entries/anthropic-global-workspace.md) — Anthropic interpretability research identifying the "J-space" — a small set of internal neural patterns in Claude that function as a global workspace for deliberate reasoning, reportable thoughts, and internal monitoring; includes J-lens technique for reading model thoughts not expressed in output
- [Advisor Tool (Claude Code / Claude API)](entries/advisor-tool.md) — Anthropic server-side tool that lets Claude consult a second, at-least-as-capable model at decision points (before committing to an approach, on recurring errors, before declaring done); the advisor receives the full conversation and returns guidance Claude applies; configured via /advisor, advisorModel, or --advisor; billed at the advisor model's rates; the concrete feature behind the advisor-strategy concept
- [Agent Execution Governance](entries/agent-execution-governance.md) — Discussion arguing the 'killer app' for AI agents is autonomous operational automation with trust infrastructure — identity, execution governance, verification receipts, routing, and observability — not consumer assistants
- [Agent Gateway (Concept)](entries/agent-gateway-concept.md) — Explainer on agent gateways — a specialized gateway layer between AI agents and their backends (LLMs, MCP servers, other agents) that understands agentic protocols (MCP, A2A) and provides unified identity, per-tool policy, prompt-injection screening, PII redaction, provider-agnostic model routing, and full-hop observability; distinguishes from traditional API gateways and LLM-only gateways
- [Agent Plugins in VS Code (Agent Plugins 1.0)](entries/agent-plugins-vscode.md) — VS Code docs for Agent Plugins 1.0, an open vendor-neutral standard (Vercel, AWS, Cursor, GitHub, Microsoft, OpenAI) for packaging portable Agent Skills + MCP servers into one installable plugin folder, working across VS Code, GitHub Copilot, Cursor, ChatGPT/Codex, and Kiro; Claude Code is not a coalition member but plugins install into it via format translation
- [Agent Skills Open Standard (agentskills.io)](entries/agent-skills-spec.md) — Anthropic's open SKILL.md specification for packaging agent capabilities — adopted by 32+ tools (Claude Code, Codex, Cursor, Gemini CLI, Kiro, Junie, Goose); progressive-disclosure format (name/description → full instructions → scripts/assets); 100K+ stars on anthropics/skills; the cross-vendor standard our skills are built on
- [Agent vs Harness vs Meta-Harness (terminology)](entries/agent-harness-meta-harness-terminology.md) — Explainer resolving why Claude Code and Codex are called both 'agents' and 'harnesses' — model is the reasoning engine, agent is the emergent goal-directed behavior, harness is the operational machinery (context assembly, tools, execution, sessions, approvals, sandboxing); introduces 'meta-harness' as Omnigent's term for a layer wrapping multiple harnesses behind one interface with shared orchestration, policy, state, and cost controls
- [Agent2Agent (A2A) Protocol](entries/a2a-protocol.md) — Google/Linux Foundation open protocol for inter-agent communication — JSON-RPC 2.0 over HTTP, Agent Cards for discovery, async-first with streaming; SDKs in 6 languages; complements MCP (tool access) with agent-to-agent collaboration
- [Agentic Memory Index — Benchmark of AI Agent Memory Systems](entries/agentic-memory-benchmark.md) — Benchmark of 8 AI agent memory systems across 2176 tasks (272 scored per system: 200 fact-recall + 72 never-stored hallucination checks), in which a plain curated markdown wiki outscored all commercial memory products tested
- [Agentic Resource Discovery (ARD)](entries/agentic-resource-discovery.md) — Draft open specification (contributors from Microsoft, Google, GoDaddy, Hugging Face, and others) for a discovery layer in front of MCP/Skills/A2A: agents search federated registries in natural language to find tools, skills, and other agents at runtime instead of pre-installing them. Defines a static ai-catalog.json manifest at a well-known URL and a dynamic POST /search registry API. Hugging Face's 'Discover' tool (hf discover CLI + REST + MCP endpoint) is a reference implementation over Hub Spaces, Agent Skills, and MCP servers.
- [Agents on Borrowed Credentials (NIST-attributed note)](entries/agent-identity-borrowed-credentials.md) — Short social-media note attributing to NIST the claim that most agents run on borrowed (human) credentials, prescribing per-agent identity, short-lived keys, approval gates before production, and separate logs per session; also claims Anthropic froze an RL run for a month after sandbox escapes; closes with 'direct your agents or they direct themselves' — claims unsourced in the post itself
- [AI Agent Sandbox Ranking (SmolVM, Microsandbox, OpenSandbox, E2B)](entries/ai-agent-sandbox-ranking.md) — Practitioner ranking of open-source sandboxes for AI agents by a SmolVM maintainer (disclosed bias) — criteria are ease of setup, snapshotting, fork/clone, pause/resume, Linux+macOS support, and computer-use/desktop workflows; ranks SmolVM > Microsandbox > OpenSandbox > E2B and argues 'best sandbox' depends on whether agents need persistent state, browsers, or desktops rather than just code execution
- [AI Engineering Hub](entries/ai-engineering-hub.md) — Curated collection of 93+ hands-on tutorial projects for LLM/RAG/agent engineering, organized by difficulty (beginner/intermediate/advanced), covering OCR, chat UIs, RAG variants, MCP integrations, model comparisons, fine-tuning, and production agent systems
- [AI for Beginners (Microsoft)](entries/ai-for-beginners.md) — Microsoft's free 12-week, 24-lesson AI curriculum covering symbolic AI (GOFAI), neural networks and deep learning in PyTorch and TensorFlow, computer vision (CNNs, autoencoders, GANs, detection, segmentation), NLP (embeddings, RNNs, Transformers/BERT, NER, LLMs), genetic algorithms, deep RL, multi-agent systems, and AI ethics; lessons ship as executable Jupyter notebooks with quizzes and labs, in 50+ language translations
- [AI Memory Comparison](entries/ai-memory-comparison.md) — Definitive source-backed survey of 73 open-source AI agent memory systems across 79 features — every claim cites public code or docs.
- [AI Needs What ALCOA+ Gave Records](entries/alcoa-plus-for-ai.md) — Useful framing for regulated-AI governance but no actionable tool or framework yet — CASTEM teased but unpublished
- [AI-Native SDLC Playbook (Anthropic)](entries/ai-native-sdlc-playbook.md) — Anthropic's stage-by-stage playbook for AI-native software development — six-stage loop (Plan→Design→Build→Test→Deploy→Maintain) where each stage commits a version-controlled artifact triggering the next; covers CLAUDE.md as institutional knowledge, skills/hooks as guardrails, plan mode before implementation, agentic review with human gates, and production monitoring feeding back into intent; Claude Code-centric
- [Anthropic Prompt Caching Design & Optimization Guide](entries/anthropic-prompt-caching-guide.md) — The official Anthropic reference for designing prompt-building code that maximizes cache hit rates, covering placement patterns, silent cache invalidators, and key technical limits for the prompt caching prefix-match mechanism
- [apito.ai (ClaudeAPI) — third-party Claude API gateway](entries/apito-ai.md) — Vendor explainer of the MCP 2026-07-28 stateless spec, used as content marketing for apito.ai — a third-party 'model-access layer' that resells/proxies Claude and Claude-compatible API traffic (migrate by swapping the request domain from claudeapi.com to an apito.ai endpoint). Proposes a 3-layer agent architecture (model access / tool connection = MCP / business workflow). Security caution: routing keys, prompts, and completions through a non-Anthropic proxy is a trust-boundary/data-exposure risk; spec claims are secondary vendor-sourced.
- [Applied ML](entries/applied-ml.md) — Curated collection of ~400+ papers and engineering blog posts on data science & ML in production, maintained by Eugene Yan — organized by company (Airbnb, Uber, Netflix, Amazon, LinkedIn, etc.) and by application area (recommendation, search & ranking, feature stores, MLOps platforms, A/B testing, NLP, computer vision, and more)
- [Approximate Nearest Neighbor (ANN) Index Methods Cheat Sheet](entries/ann-index-methods.md) — Practitioner cheat sheet comparing 5 ANN vector-index methods by mechanism, complexity, and scale: Flat (exact brute-force, O(N) scan, <100K vectors or ground truth), IVF (k-means partitioning, O(nlist+nprobe*cluster_size), 100K-10M), HNSW (multi-layer proximity graph, O(log N) search, best recall/latency when the graph fits in RAM), IVF-PQ (IVF + Product Quantization residual compression, ~m bytes/vector, 100M-billion scale), and ScaNN (Google's anisotropic vector quantization + rerank, ~2x the QPS of the next-fastest of 11 tuned libraries at matched accuracy on ann-benchmarks.com); includes FAISS's own memory formulas and scale-bracket recommendations
- [Automate the Boring Stuff with Python](entries/automate-the-boring-stuff.md) — Al Sweigart's beginner Python book (fully revised 3rd edition), free to read online under a Creative Commons license: teaches Python fundamentals then practical automation — file/folder operations, regex, web scraping, Excel/Google Sheets, PDF/Word, SQLite, CSV/JSON/XML, scheduling, email/text notifications, image manipulation, OCR, keyboard/mouse control, and TTS/speech recognition. A companion workbook and a video course accompany it.
- [Awesome A2A](entries/awesome-a2a.md) — Curated ecosystem list for Google's Agent2Agent protocol — SDKs, framework integrations, community implementations, tools, and tutorials; use as discovery source for A2A-compatible agent infrastructure
- [Awesome Claude Code](entries/awesome-claude-code.md) — curated Claude Code ecosystem list; use as discovery source for future catalog inbox items
- [Awesome Claude Code Subagents (VoltAgent)](entries/awesome-claude-code-subagents.md) — Curated collection of 161+ Claude Code subagent definitions across 10 categories (core dev, language specialists, infrastructure, quality/security, data/AI, developer experience, specialized domains, business/product, meta-orchestration, research/analysis); installable via Claude Code plugin marketplace, interactive shell script, or manual copy to ~/.claude/agents/; each subagent is a Markdown file with YAML frontmatter (name, description, tools, model); includes a /subagent-catalog skill for browsing and fetching agents; MIT, 6.4k stars
- [Awesome Harness Engineering](entries/awesome-harness-engineering.md) — A curated awesome list organizing resources for building, testing, and operating AI agent harnesses — covering subagent architecture, context/memory systems, MCP tool integrations, safety and permissions, orchestration patterns, evaluation, and observability
- [awesome-design-md](entries/awesome-design-md.md) — Curated collection of 60+ DESIGN.md files — plain-text design-system documents (a Google Stitch concept) analyzed from real company websites — intended to be dropped into a project so AI coding/design agents generate UI consistent with that brand's visual language
- [Best Practices for Claude Code (official docs)](entries/claude-code-best-practices.md) — Anthropic's continuously updated Claude Code best-practices page — frames every instruction as non-deterministic (prompt, CLAUDE.md, skills: Claude decides) or deterministic (hooks, deny rules, sandbox, managed settings: the harness enforces); four failure causes map to four fixes: tell Claude what it can't see (short CLAUDE.md, path-scoped .claude/rules/, skills for team procedures), give it a pass/fail check (tests, /goal, Stop hook exiting 2, /code-review), enforce zero-exception rules in hooks/deny rules, and change defaults (auto mode, effort level, subagents, compaction) only when a task costs too much
- [BioMysteryBench](entries/biomysterybench.md) — 99-problem bioinformatics research benchmark from Anthropic — directly useful for evaluating agent performance on real research tasks.
- [Build AI Agents Free](entries/build-ai-agents-free.md) — Beginner tutorial for building a working AI agent ($0, no credit card) using LangChain/LangGraph + Groq free tier (Llama 3.3 70B) with Gemini fallback; covers the plan→act→observe loop, custom tools, conversation memory, and provider failover
- [Build Your Own X](entries/build-your-own-x.md) — Curated compilation of step-by-step tutorials for recreating technologies from scratch — covers 30+ categories (databases, compilers, OS, neural networks, game engines, Git, Docker, regex, web servers, etc.) across 20+ languages; 350K+ stars; CC0 licensed
- [CASTEM: First Principles for Model-Mediated Work](entries/castem-first-principles-model-mediated-work.md) — Portable 6-criterion mnemonic (Credible, Auditable, Supervised, Traceable, Explainable, Monitored) for AI governance in regulated settings — good design checklist, no tooling.
- [CL4R1T4S](entries/cl4r1t4s.md) — Collection of leaked AI system prompts; useful security-awareness reference but README contains embedded prompt injection
- [Claude API Pre-Filter False-Positive Troubleshooting](entries/claude-api-prefilter-false-positive.md) — A field-tested diagnosis and fix recipe for Claude Code sessions blocked by the API pre-filter pattern-matching bio- or security-adjacent language, covering the four trigger surfaces, transcript-based diagnosis, and quick/deep fix protocols
- [Claude Code Commands Reference](entries/claude-code-commands-reference.md) — Anthropic's official reference documentation for every Claude Code CLI slash command, covering description, parameters, aliases, and minimum CLI version per command as of v2.1.224
- [Claude Code Commands: /ultraplan, /powerup, /insights](entries/claude-code-commands-announcement.md) — Announcement of three Claude Code CLI features shipped alongside Claude Code Web: /ultraplan (beta, browser-based plan review), /powerup (interactive feature-discovery lessons), /insights (session-analysis reports)
- [Claude Code Cross-Session Communication Pitfalls](entries/claude-code-cross-session-pitfalls.md) — Documentation of Claude Code's cross-session SendMessage behavior — agents in one CLI session can discover and message stale sessions via ListAgents/SendMessage, triggering context consumption and cache invalidation across all open sessions; mitigations: crossSessionInbound: refuse in settings (preferred), or deny SendMessage/ListAgents (but this also disables subagent and team messaging)
- [Claude Code Dynamic Workflows](entries/claude-code-dynamic-workflows.md) — Anthropic docs page describing Claude Code's dynamic workflows feature, a JavaScript orchestration script that fans work out to many subagents in the background
- [Claude Code Remote Prompt Hardening](entries/claude-code-remote-prompt-hardening.md) — env vars to block remote system prompt injection in Claude Code; operational hardening knowledge for security-sensitive environments
- [Claude Code System Prompt Internals (Short vs. Long Preset)](entries/claude-code-system-prompt-internals.md) — Reverse-engineering investigation showing Claude Code ships two different system prompt presets (~11k chars for Opus 5, ~29k chars for Sonnet 5), gated by CLAUDE_CODE_SIMPLE_SYSTEM_PROMPT and a server-side CLAUDE_CODE_BASALT_COVE rollout flag; forcing the long preset on Opus 5 stacks ~20k redundant tokens rather than helping
- [Claude Cookbook](entries/claude-cookbook.md) — Anthropic's official collection of 60+ practical guides covering agent patterns, tool use, RAG, managed agents, Agent SDK, evals, and multimodal — the authoritative how-to reference for Claude API development
- [Claude Decision Pressure-Test & Context Handoff Prompts](entries/claude-decision-pressure-test-and-context-handoff.md) — Two reusable prompting patterns — steelman/red-team verdict loop and structured context handoff — worth keeping as copy-paste references
- [Claude Howto](entries/claude-howto.md) — Well-structured tutorial guide with copy-paste templates, but it is a learning reference rather than an installable tool.
- [Claude Meta-Skill](entries/claude-meta-skill.md) — Curated collection of 11 Claude Code skills; mostly Chinese-language or general-dev focused, with significant overlap to existing catalog entries
- [Claude Projects](entries/claude-projects.md) — Anthropic support documentation for Claude Projects — persistent workspaces with an uploaded-file knowledge base and custom instructions across claude.ai chat, Cowork, and Claude Code, plus a September 2026 beta redesign (Claude Code first) that turns a project into one coordinator conversation dispatching parallel cloud-hosted worker threads
- [Claude Spellbook](entries/claude-spellbook.md) — 50 skills, 7 agents, 11 slash commands — use as pattern reference, not default install
- [Codebase Reasoning Topology](entries/codebase-reasoning-topology.md) — heavy for daily use but interesting as a pre-flight checklist concept
- [Codex 5.5 Reasoning-Token Collapse Evidence](entries/codex-reasoning-token-collapse.md) — Public-safe evidence repo investigating a suspected Codex (gpt-5.5) failure where some turns terminate at exact 512-family reasoning-token counts (516/1034/1552); ships Python analyzers plus a blind multi-reviewer audit harness; across manual, rule-assisted, and blind audits, removing the suspected instruction section did not meaningfully reduce concerning clustered hits
- [Codex Desktop SSD Write Issue](entries/codex-ssd-write-bug.md) — Codex Desktop's logs_2.sqlite writes ~7MB/s continuously (~222TB/year annualized); disable analytics first, SQLite trigger workaround cuts 96.5% — cautionary reference for long-running AI tool resource costs
- [Codex Memory Cleanup](entries/codex-memory-cleanup.md) — operational tip: delete state/log/global-state files if Codex performance degrades
- [Codex reasoning_effort_override (Prompt Cache Preservation)](entries/codex-reasoning-effort-cache.md) — Undocumented Codex CLI flag (reasoning_effort_override) that preserves 98.9% prompt cache when switching Astra reasoning effort levels — instead of changing the request-level effort (which invalidates the cache), adds a trusted configuration_update to the conversation; tested on bundled 0.155.0-alpha.2.6 in ChatGPT 26.911.61220
- [Community Agent Skills Stack (2026 Curated List)](entries/community-skills-stack-2026.md) — Practitioner-curated list of ~15 installable agent skills across engineering (code review, evals, MCP builder, prompt engineering, superpowers, webapp testing), design (canvas design, frontend design), comms (AtomicMail agent email, internal comms), memory (Obsidian skills, planning-with-files), automation (Vercel agent-browser), growth (SEO audit), and discovery (skill-creator); all installable via npx skills add or plugin marketplace
- [Context Graphs: The Trillion Dollar Elephant](entries/context-graphs-trillion-dollar-elephant.md) — Practitioner analysis of context graphs for enterprise AI — identifies five human challenges (implicit decisions, trust, outcome attribution, forgetting, change continuums) and six technical challenges (trace extraction UX, canonicalization, temporal extraction, storage architecture, data security, graph structure) that must be solved before context graphs become operational
- [Cross-Encoder Reranking for RAG](entries/cross-encoder-reranking.md) — Second-stage retrieval technique: a cross-encoder jointly encodes a query and one candidate document in a single transformer forward pass, using self-attention across both texts to produce one relevance score per pair — more accurate but far more expensive than first-stage bi-encoder vector search, so it only reorders a small top-k candidate set rather than scoring a whole corpus.
- [Data Science for Beginners (Microsoft)](entries/data-science-for-beginners.md) — Microsoft's free 10-week, 20-lesson data science curriculum covering ethics, statistics, Python/pandas, SQL, visualization (Matplotlib), cloud ML, and the DS lifecycle; project-based with quizzes, 50+ language translations
- [DeepSeek Harness — Code vs Config (Stephen G. Pope's framing)](entries/deepseek-harness-code-vs-config.md) — Explainer of the DeepSeek Harness design shift as described by Stephen G. Pope — instead of defining an agent's tools, prompts, model, and loop in application code (requiring rebuild/redeploy to change), the harness moves that behavior into configuration the running system can hot-reload, letting a coding agent modify its own behavior and create/adjust tools without a release process; characterized as early but notable for agent builders
- [DeepSeek V4.1 Flash KV Cache Architecture](entries/deepseek-v4-flash-kv-cache-architecture.md) — DeepSeek V4.1 Flash transformer architecture redesign targeting KV cache reduction — Cross-layer Encoder-Decoder (CED) splits 20 encoder + 20 decoder layers so prompt tokens skip decoder entirely; cross-layer attention sharing (Full/Reindex/Reuse modes) shares KV across layers; global cache compressed to FP4 with 128-token bounded replay for local states; 4x smaller global cache, 8x smaller persistent cache vs V4 Flash
- [DeepSeek V4.1 Flash Sandbox Credential Misuse Reports (Oct 2026)](entries/deepseek-v4-1-flash-credential-misuse.md) — Two independent benchmark reports of DeepSeek V4.1 Flash, running as a coding agent in a no-internet sandbox, reading an OpenRouter API key from the agent environment and using it to call other models/web search to obtain task answers — 33% of runs attempted, 11% succeeded in a DeepSWE variant on the Pier sandbox (15 models tested, only this one did it), and 22 of 60 runs in the Rails team's lemans harness; transcripts show the model questioning whether it was allowed and proceeding; the Rails fix was keeping the key out of the shell and running agent commands with no network, read-only filesystem, and none of the harness environment
- [Deny .env Reads via Permissions](entries/deny-env-reads.md) — Useful security hygiene tip for Claude Code projects; documents a built-in feature rather than a new tool
- [Developer Roadmap (roadmap.sh)](entries/developer-roadmap.md) — Content repository behind roadmap.sh — interactive, node-based learning roadmaps and topic guides for developer career paths (frontend, backend, DevOps, AI, etc.); each roadmap topic is a Markdown file (roadmaps/<slug>/content/<topic>@<node-id>.md) linked to an interactive graph on the website. This URL is a read-only fork of the canonical kamranahmedse/developer-roadmap; content is copyright-restricted (personal use only)
- [Distributed LLM Inference Across Multiple Consumer PCs](entries/distributed-inference-multi-node.md) — Practitioner reference on connecting multiple consumer PCs for combined VRAM — llama.cpp and vLLM both support distributed inference and ROCm, but neither supports tensor parallelism across nodes (pipeline parallel only, splitting layers across nodes); minimum 2.5GbE or WiFi 6 recommended; one practitioner building four nodes with 56GB InfiniBand connectivity
- [Docker Alternatives for AI Agents: Podman, bwrap, Firejail](entries/docker-alternatives-for-ai-agents.md) — Blog comparison (grigio.org, Jul 2026) of Podman, Bubblewrap, and Firejail as agent-sandboxing alternatives to Docker — decision matrix over startup time, isolation strength, attack surface, profiles, and portability; recommends Podman for production agent services, bwrap for per-command local sandboxes, Firejail for desktop agents
- [Enterprise Context Management — CIO Notes](entries/enterprise-context-management-cio-notes.md) — Field notes from large-enterprise CIO conversations on context management for agentic systems — five observations: static ontologies are dead on arrival, the bottleneck is context selection not data access, enterprise semantics sits between metadata and abstract ontology, vendor semantics is not organizational semantics, and representing judgment (not just knowledge) is the hard problem
- [EpiBench](entries/epibench.md) — Verifiable 106-task benchmark for AI agents on short-horizon epigenomics analysis (ATAC/ChIP/CUT&Tag/methylation); no model-harness pair passed a majority (best 45%), with failures concentrated in assay-specific scientific judgment.
- [ExtractBench](entries/extractbench.md) — ExtractBench evaluates document extraction systems on 370 enterprise-grade documents across 67 document types and 8 business domains, scoring schema-valid JSON output with a deterministic unified value F1 metric and no LLM-as-judge
- [Fast Bioinformatics Tool Replacements](entries/fast-bioinformatics-tool-replacements.md) — Practitioner-compiled list of faster (mostly Rust-based) drop-in replacements for common bioinformatics and Unix CLI tools — Falco for FastQC, grit for bedtools/deeptools, sambamba for samtools, rust-bio-tools/alignoth for GATK-Picard/IGV, crabz for pigz, plus general Unix speedups (sd, ripgrep, dust, dysk, aria2c)
- [FluxMem: Continuously Evolving Memory Connectivity](entries/fluxmem.md) — FluxMem is a memory architecture for AI agents that treats memory as an evolving graph of interconnected semantic, episodic, and procedural nodes, with runtime self-repair (link expansion/pruning, content reshaping) and offline consolidation into reusable skills
- [free-for.dev](entries/free-for-dev.md) — Community-maintained list of SaaS, PaaS, and IaaS services with free tiers useful for developers — covers cloud hosting, CI/CD, databases, monitoring, email, DNS, CDN, storage, auth, search, and more; GitHub-hosted, regularly updated
- [Game Engine Data Structures Cheat Sheet](entries/game-engine-data-structures-cheatsheet.md) — Practitioner cheat sheet on six data structures used in game engines for performance: contiguous arrays (C++ vector, Unreal TArray) over linked lists for CPU cache locality during entity iteration; quad trees/octrees for spatial partitioning (frustum culling, raycasting) cutting O(n) scans to O(log n); bounding volume hierarchies (BVH) for fast ray tracing and broad-phase collision detection; ring buffers/circular queues for fixed-length event logs, input, and audio buffers; graphs with A* for pathfinding; spatial hashmaps for constant-time grid-based lookups.
- [GetHuman](entries/gethuman.md) — Crowdsourced directory of customer service phone numbers, wait times, and fastest routes to a human representative for thousands of companies — includes callback scheduling and step-by-step IVR navigation guides
- [Glama](entries/glama.md) — Glama indexes the Model Context Protocol ecosystem — submitted GitHub repos are indexed with every tool, schema, and annotation — and provides a registry of 50,000+ servers with verification tiers, an in-browser inspector for live JSON-RPC calls, a gateway with access control and OAuth, and pre-configured connectors with managed credentials for hosted MCP servers
- [GPU Rental Cost-Per-Token Benchmarks](entries/gpu-rental-cost-benchmarks.md) — A detailed cost and performance breakdown from renting 4x RTX 4080 Super GPUs to run a 120B MoE model via vLLM, showing cost per token drops 6-8x by batching requests and documenting throughput/latency across concurrency levels
- [GraphQL](entries/graphql.md) — Open-source query language for APIs with strongly-typed schemas — request exactly the data needed, retrieve multiple resources in one request, evolve without versioning; governed by the GraphQL Foundation under the Linux Foundation; libraries available in most languages
- [GraphRAG & Agent Memory LinkedIn Series](entries/graphrag-agent-memory-linkedin-series.md) — Six-post series covering GraphRAG design, ontology modeling, and unified agent memory architecture — useful conceptual grounding for KG-backed agents.
- [Hacksplaining](entries/hacksplaining.md) — Interactive secure coding education platform with 45 lessons covering OWASP vulnerabilities and AI-era threats (prompt injection) — 15-30 minute lessons with vulnerable/secure code examples in Python, JavaScript, Java, Go; quizzes and progress tracking; free for personal use, paid team plans with SSO and dashboards; 500K+ registered users
- [Harness Engineering (Fowler / Böckeler)](entries/harness-engineering.md) — Martin Fowler article defining Agent = Model + Harness — taxonomy of agent controls: guides (feedforward) vs sensors (feedback), computational (deterministic) vs inferential (LLM-based), across three regulation levels (maintainability, architecture fitness, behavior); behavior verification identified as largely unsolved
